===== FILE: README.MD =====
(match #1, match line: 1, block lines: 1-26, block type: context, matched by: colbert)

     1: # Patent Wizard Backend: Strict 128-d ColBERT Pipeline
     2: 
     3: This backend is now hard-locked to:
     4: - `TOKEN_VECTOR_DIM=128`
     5: - `PROJECTION_MODE=trained`
     6: - trained projection weights loaded from `PROJECTION_PATH`
     7: 
     8: There is no 768 fallback path and no random projection path.
     9: 
    10: ## Required Environment Configuration
    11: 
    12: Set these before running ingest/retrieval:
    13: 
    14: ```powershell
    15: $env:TOKEN_VECTOR_DIM="128"
    16: $env:PROJECTION_MODE="trained"
    17: $env:PROJECTION_PATH="C:\path\to\trained_projection.pt"   # required
    18: $env:TOKEN_VECTOR_DTYPE="float16"                         # float16 or float32
    19: $env:COLBERT_VARIANTS="128_f16,128_f32"
    20: $env:LMDB_WRITE_VARIANTS="128_f16"
    21: $env:WEAVIATE_NAMED_VECTOR="colbert"
    22: ```
    23: 
    24: Validation happens at import/startup:
    25: - projection file must exist
    26: - projection matrix shape must be `[128,768]` or `[768,128]` (transposed explicitly)

===== FILE: docs/system_overview.md =====
(match #11, match line: 22, block lines: 1-47, block type: context, matched by: colbert)

     1: # Patent Wizard – System Overview
     2: 
     3: ## 1. Data Ingestion & Chunking (`backend/app/services/download.py`)
     4: 
     5: 1. `bulk_dataset_download()` orchestrates pulling USPTO weekly archives (ZIP/TAR). It now accepts `batch_size` + `on_batch` so callers can stream chunk lists in manageable batches instead of holding the entire weekly dump in memory.
     6: 2. For each patent XML:
     7:    - Metadata (`doc_id`, `title`, `filing_date`, CPC classification, inventors, etc.) is extracted.
     8:    - Text sections are converted to simplified rich text via `elem_to_rich_text()` so inline formatting (e.g., `<sup>`) survives.
     9:    - `_fig_refs_in_elem()` identifies figure references in description/claim paragraphs. It collects both `idref` attributes (e.g., `DRAWINGS-0001`) and any numeric mentions (“FIG. 1”), which later map to specific figure assets.
    10:    - `_collect_figure_assets()` scans `<drawings>/<figure>/<img>` tags and reads matching image files from the archive. Each figure captures filename, referenced IDs, figure number, and raw bytes.
    11:    - `_attach_fig_images_to_chunks()` takes the chunks just emitted for that patent, resolves their `fig_refs` against the collected assets, base64-encodes the image bytes, and attaches a `fig_images` array directly on each chunk.
    12: 3. Sampling helpers (`write_random_patent_sample`, `write_patent_sample_by_ids`) write per-patent Markdown folders under `uspto_bulk_data/sampled`, drop the original image files into `images/`, and emit `backend/app/db/sample_chunks.jsonl` so the demo backend has material to load.
    13: 
    14: Key helpers:
    15: 
    16: - `make_parser()` – hardened `lxml` parser (no DTD/entities, `huge_tree`, recover toggles).
    17: - `iter_uspto_subdocs()` – streaming generator that slices the massive weekly XML into discrete `<us-patent-application>` blobs without parsing.
    18: - `rich_to_plain()` – strips the minimal rich text back to plain text; used downstream by embedding, dataset catalog, and UI snippets.
    19: 
    20: ## 2. Embedding (`backend/app/embed.py`)
    21: 
    22: 1. Loads the ColBERTv2 tokenizer/model (Hugging Face) once at import.
    23: 2. `embed_chunks()` expects a list of chunk dicts. For each chunk:
    24:    - `rich_to_plain()` cleans the text.
    25:    - The tokenizer truncates/pads to `max_length` and the ColBERT model produces contextual embeddings.
    26:    - The mean of token embeddings (per chunk) becomes a dense vector saved back onto the chunk (`chunk["embedding"]`).
    27: 3. `embed_query()` mirrors the chunk pathway for user queries so retrieval can operate in the same vector space.
    28: 
    29: ## 3. Storage (`backend/app/store.py`)
    30: 
    31: 1. Uses the Weaviate Python client (`weaviate.connect_to_local`) to talk to a local vector database.
    32: 2. `ensure_collection()` guarantees the `PatentData` collection exists with schema fields: document metadata, chunk text, and `fig_images` (JSON string that carries base64 images).
    33: 3. `store_embeddings()` takes the enriched chunk dicts (now with vectors + metadata) and inserts them into Weaviate. The frontend ultimately queries through the FastAPI layer rather than directly from Weaviate in this POC, but the storage step makes the vector data available for future semantic retrieval work.
    34: 
    35: ## 4. Demo Dataset Loader (`backend/app/db/dataset.py`)
    36: 
    37: - `_load_sampled_chunks()` reads the JSONL emitted by the download step and groups chunks by `doc_id`.
    38: - `_build_catalog_from_chunks()` builds lightweight catalog entries (title, snippet, search_text, metadata) for each patent. It also collects up to six unique `fig_images` per doc so the API can surface figure previews without having to stream the full chunk list.
    39: - The resulting `CATALOG` is an in-memory list used by the naive retrieval service.
    40: 
    41: ## 5. Retrieval & API (`backend/app/services/retrieval.py`, `backend/app/api/search.py`)
    42: 
    43: 1. `naive_filter()` performs a case-insensitive substring match against `title`, `snippet`, and `search_text`. It returns Pydantic `SearchItem` objects (including `fig_images`).
    44: 2. `/api/search` (`backend/app/api/search.py`):
    45:    - Accepts `q`, `k`, `k_extra`, and `rag` flags.
    46:    - Calls `naive_filter()`, slices the top-`k` “cited” items, and exposes the rest as “other” results.
    47:    - If `rag=true` (default), it calls `make_fake_answer()` (placeholder orchestrator) to generate a mock RAG answer referencing the cited items with `[1]`, `[2]` style citations.

     1: # Patent Wizard – System Overview
     2: 
     3: ## 1. Data Ingestion & Chunking (`backend/app/services/download.py`)
     4: 
     5: 1. `bulk_dataset_download()` orchestrates pulling USPTO weekly archives (ZIP/TAR). It now accepts `batch_size` + `on_batch` so callers can stream chunk lists in manageable batches instead of holding the entire weekly dump in memory.
     6: 2. For each patent XML:
     7:    - Metadata (`doc_id`, `title`, `filing_date`, CPC classification, inventors, etc.) is extracted.
     8:    - Text sections are converted to simplified rich text via `elem_to_rich_text()` so inline formatting (e.g., `<sup>`) survives.
     9:    - `_fig_refs_in_elem()` identifies figure references in description/claim paragraphs. It collects both `idref` attributes (e.g., `DRAWINGS-0001`) and any numeric mentions (“FIG. 1”), which later map to specific figure assets.
    10:    - `_collect_figure_assets()` scans `<drawings>/<figure>/<img>` tags and reads matching image files from the archive. Each figure captures filename, referenced IDs, figure number, and raw bytes.
    11:    - `_attach_fig_images_to_chunks()` takes the chunks just emitted for that patent, resolves their `fig_refs` against the collected assets, base64-encodes the image bytes, and attaches a `fig_images` array directly on each chunk.
    12: 3. Sampling helpers (`write_random_patent_sample`, `write_patent_sample_by_ids`) write per-patent Markdown folders under `uspto_bulk_data/sampled`, drop the original image files into `images/`, and emit `backend/app/db/sample_chunks.jsonl` so the demo backend has material to load.
    13: 
    14: Key helpers:
    15: 
    16: - `make_parser()` – hardened `lxml` parser (no DTD/entities, `huge_tree`, recover toggles).
    17: - `iter_uspto_subdocs()` – streaming generator that slices the massive weekly XML into discrete `<us-patent-application>` blobs without parsing.
    18: - `rich_to_plain()` – strips the minimal rich text back to plain text; used downstream by embedding, dataset catalog, and UI snippets.
    19: 
    20: ## 2. Embedding (`backend/app/embed.py`)
    21: 
    22: 1. Loads the ColBERTv2 tokenizer/model (Hugging Face) once at import.
    23: 2. `embed_chunks()` expects a list of chunk dicts. For each chunk:
    24:    - `rich_to_plain()` cleans the text.
    25:    - The tokenizer truncates/pads to `max_length` and the ColBERT model produces contextual embeddings.
    26:    - The mean of token embeddings (per chunk) becomes a dense vector saved back onto the chunk (`chunk["embedding"]`).
    27: 3. `embed_query()` mirrors the chunk pathway for user queries so retrieval can operate in the same vector space.
    28: 
    29: ## 3. Storage (`backend/app/store.py`)
    30: 
    31: 1. Uses the Weaviate Python client (`weaviate.connect_to_local`) to talk to a local vector database.
    32: 2. `ensure_collection()` guarantees the `PatentData` collection exists with schema fields: document metadata, chunk text, and `fig_images` (JSON string that carries base64 images).
    33: 3. `store_embeddings()` takes the enriched chunk dicts (now with vectors + metadata) and inserts them into Weaviate. The frontend ultimately queries through the FastAPI layer rather than directly from Weaviate in this POC, but the storage step makes the vector data available for future semantic retrieval work.
    34: 
    35: ## 4. Demo Dataset Loader (`backend/app/db/dataset.py`)
    36: 
    37: - `_load_sampled_chunks()` reads the JSONL emitted by the download step and groups chunks by `doc_id`.
    38: - `_build_catalog_from_chunks()` builds lightweight catalog entries (title, snippet, search_text, metadata) for each patent. It also collects up to six unique `fig_images` per doc so the API can surface figure previews without having to stream the full chunk list.
    39: - The resulting `CATALOG` is an in-memory list used by the naive retrieval service.
    40: 
    41: ## 5. Retrieval & API (`backend/app/services/retrieval.py`, `backend/app/api/search.py`)
    42: 
    43: 1. `naive_filter()` performs a case-insensitive substring match against `title`, `snippet`, and `search_text`. It returns Pydantic `SearchItem` objects (including `fig_images`).
    44: 2. `/api/search` (`backend/app/api/search.py`):
    45:    - Accepts `q`, `k`, `k_extra`, and `rag` flags.
    46:    - Calls `naive_filter()`, slices the top-`k` “cited” items, and exposes the rest as “other” results.
    47:    - If `rag=true` (default), it calls `make_fake_answer()` (placeholder orchestrator) to generate a mock RAG answer referencing the cited items with `[1]`, `[2]` style citations.
    48:    - Returns a `SearchResponse` envelope that the frontend expects.
    49: 
    50: ## 6. Frontend (`frontend/src`)

===== FILE: DESIGN_CHOICES.md =====
(match #15, match line: 4, block lines: 1-29, block type: context, matched by: colbert)

     1: # Design Choices
     2: 
     3: ## LMDB Storage Format
     4: Decision: Store ColBERT token vectors as NumPy `.npy` bytes (uncompressed).
     5: Rationale: Self-describing dtype/shape with reasonable overhead; simpler to decode correctly.
     6: Alternatives:
     7: - Raw float32 bytes
     8:   - Pros: Fastest, smallest overhead.
     9:   - Cons: Must store/track dtype + shape separately; easier to misdecode.
    10: - JSON
    11:   - Pros: Human-readable.
    12:   - Cons: Huge size, slow IO/parse, not practical for large vectors.
    13: 
    14: ## LMDB Compression
    15: Decision: No compression initially.
    16: Rationale: Highest retrieval quality and simplest pipeline; measure performance first.
    17: Alternatives:
    18: - Float16
    19:   - Pros: ~2x smaller, typically minimal quality loss.
    20:   - Cons: Some precision loss; extra conversion step.
    21: - INT8 / PQ
    22:   - Pros: 4x-16x smaller.
    23:   - Cons: More quality loss, higher complexity.
    24: 
    25: ## Token Dimensionality Reduction (Experimental)
    26: Decision: When producing 128-d token vectors, apply an explicit linear projection
    27: from 768 -> 128 with no bias, then L2-normalize per token.
    28: Rationale: Provides a deterministic, explicit reduction step without assuming
    29: model-specific ColBERT heads or hidden configs.

===== FILE: DESIGN_CHOICES.md =====
(match #16, match line: 29, block lines: 4-54, block type: context, matched by: colbert)

     4: Decision: Store ColBERT token vectors as NumPy `.npy` bytes (uncompressed).
     5: Rationale: Self-describing dtype/shape with reasonable overhead; simpler to decode correctly.
     6: Alternatives:
     7: - Raw float32 bytes
     8:   - Pros: Fastest, smallest overhead.
     9:   - Cons: Must store/track dtype + shape separately; easier to misdecode.
    10: - JSON
    11:   - Pros: Human-readable.
    12:   - Cons: Huge size, slow IO/parse, not practical for large vectors.
    13: 
    14: ## LMDB Compression
    15: Decision: No compression initially.
    16: Rationale: Highest retrieval quality and simplest pipeline; measure performance first.
    17: Alternatives:
    18: - Float16
    19:   - Pros: ~2x smaller, typically minimal quality loss.
    20:   - Cons: Some precision loss; extra conversion step.
    21: - INT8 / PQ
    22:   - Pros: 4x-16x smaller.
    23:   - Cons: More quality loss, higher complexity.
    24: 
    25: ## Token Dimensionality Reduction (Experimental)
    26: Decision: When producing 128-d token vectors, apply an explicit linear projection
    27: from 768 -> 128 with no bias, then L2-normalize per token.
    28: Rationale: Provides a deterministic, explicit reduction step without assuming
    29: model-specific ColBERT heads or hidden configs.
    30: Notes:
    31: - The projection layer is initialized once and reused.
    32: - This is a temporary stand-in for a true ColBERT head and can be swapped later.
    33: 
    34: ## Token Vector Variants
    35: Decision: Store four LMDB variants per claim ID:
    36: - 768_f32
    37: - 768_f16
    38: - 128_f32
    39: - 128_f16
    40: Rationale: Enables empirical comparisons of size/quality tradeoffs without
    41: re-ingesting claims.
    42: 
    43: ## Model Dimension Alignment (Weaviate/MUVERA)
    44: Decision: Force the same 768-dim ColBERT model at both ingest and retrieval.
    45: Rationale: Weaviate's vector index expects a fixed dimensionality; mixing
    46: models (e.g., 256-dim vs 768-dim) causes nearVector dimension mismatch errors.
    47: Implementation note:
    48: - Set `COLBERT_MODEL_NAME=colbert-ir/colbertv2.0` (and `MODEL_NAME` to match)
    49:   before ingest and before retrieval scripts/GUI.
    50: 
    51: ## Optional LMDB Writes
    52: Decision: Allow disabling LMDB shard creation via `WRITE_LMDB=0`.
    53: Rationale: Teammates can ingest into Weaviate without copying large LMDB shards.
    54:

    55: ## LMDB Keying Strategy
    56: Decision: Use the Weaviate object UUID as the LMDB key.
    57: Rationale: Direct mapping between Weaviate chunk records and ColBERT vectors.
    58: Alternatives:
    59: - `doc_id:chunk_index`
    60:   - Pros: Human-readable, deterministic.
    61:   - Cons: Diverges from Weaviate IDs; requires extra mapping layer.
    62: - Separate `chunk_id`
    63:   - Pros: Explicit and portable.
    64:   - Cons: Another ID to generate/maintain.
    65: 
    66: ## LMDB Sharding
    67: Decision: Single LMDB file to start.
    68: Rationale: Simpler to manage and integrate; sharding can be added when corpus grows.

    57: Rationale: Direct mapping between Weaviate chunk records and ColBERT vectors.
    58: Alternatives:
    59: - `doc_id:chunk_index`
    60:   - Pros: Human-readable, deterministic.

    62: - Separate `chunk_id`
    63:   - Pros: Explicit and portable.
    64:   - Cons: Another ID to generate/maintain.
    65: 
    66: ## LMDB Sharding
    67: Decision: Single LMDB file to start.
    68: Rationale: Simpler to manage and integrate; sharding can be added when corpus grows.
    69: Alternatives:
    70: - Per-shard LMDB (e.g., by hash prefix or date range)
    71:   - Pros: Smaller files, better cache locality, easier partial rebuilds.
    72:   - Cons: Routing logic, more handles, more operational complexity.
    73: 
    74: ## Write Stage
    75: Decision: Write LMDB entries during the Weaviate store step.
    76: Rationale: Weaviate UUID is available at insert time; keeps embedding and storage concerns separated.
    77: Alternatives:
    78: - Write during embedding
    79:   - Pros: Immediate persistence while vectors are in memory.
    80:   - Cons: Requires stable ID earlier; tighter coupling in the pipeline.
    81: 
    82: ## Target LMDB Location

(match #22, match line: 92, block lines: 67-117, block type: context, matched by: colbert)

    67: Decision: Single LMDB file to start.
    68: Rationale: Simpler to manage and integrate; sharding can be added when corpus grows.
    69: Alternatives:
    70: - Per-shard LMDB (e.g., by hash prefix or date range)
    71:   - Pros: Smaller files, better cache locality, easier partial rebuilds.
    72:   - Cons: Routing logic, more handles, more operational complexity.
    73: 
    74: ## Write Stage
    75: Decision: Write LMDB entries during the Weaviate store step.
    76: Rationale: Weaviate UUID is available at insert time; keeps embedding and storage concerns separated.
    77: Alternatives:
    78: - Write during embedding
    79:   - Pros: Immediate persistence while vectors are in memory.
    80:   - Cons: Requires stable ID earlier; tighter coupling in the pipeline.
    81: 
    82: ## Target LMDB Location
    83: Decision: Use `backend/lmdb/`.
    84: Rationale: Clear top-level data location, separate from app code.
    85: Alternatives:
    86: - `backend/app/lmdb/`
    87:   - Pros: Co-locates with application code.
    88:   - Cons: Mixes data with code.
    89: 
    90: ## Retrieval Workflow (Target)
    91: 1) Download and chunk
    92: 2) Embed with ColBERT
    93: 3) Weaviate applies MUVERA encoding to multi-vector field at index time
    94: 4) Retrieve Weaviate UUIDs and load ColBERT vectors from LMDB by UUID
    95: # MUVERA multi-vector indexing (Weaviate)
    96: Weaviate’s MUVERA expects **multi-vector** inputs and encodes them internally into fixed-length vectors for indexing.
    97: 
    98: Doc-aligned config (local client):
    99:

   101: from weaviate.classes.config import Configure
   102: 
   103: client.collections.create(
   104:     name="PatentData",
   105:     properties=[...],
   106:     vector_config=[
   107:         Configure.MultiVectors.self_provided(

   119:     properties={...},
   120:     vector={"colbert": multi_vector_colbert},
   121: )
   122: ```
   123: 
   124: Notes:
   125: - You must send **multi‑vector ColBERT** at ingest **and** query time.
   126: - MUVERA encodes internally; you don’t store a separate “muvera” vector.
   127: 
   128: Weaviate batching
   129: - `store.py` uses `collection.data.insert_many` to batch inserts.
   130: - Batch size is controlled by `WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE` (default 128).

    95: # MUVERA multi-vector indexing (Weaviate)
    96: Weaviate’s MUVERA expects **multi-vector** inputs and encodes them internally into fixed-length vectors for indexing.
    97: 
    98: Doc-aligned config (local client):
    99: 
   100: ```python
   101: from weaviate.classes.config import Configure
   102: 
   103: client.collections.create(
   104:     name="PatentData",
   105:     properties=[...],
   106:     vector_config=[
   107:         Configure.MultiVectors.self_provided(

   119:     properties={...},
   120:     vector={"colbert": multi_vector_colbert},
   121: )
   122: ```
   123: 
   124: Notes:
   125: - You must send **multi‑vector ColBERT** at ingest **and** query time.
   126: - MUVERA encodes internally; you don’t store a separate “muvera” vector.
   127: 
   128: Weaviate batching
   129: - `store.py` uses `collection.data.insert_many` to batch inserts.
   130: - Batch size is controlled by `WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE` (default 128).
   131: 
   132: LMDB ColBERT storage
   133: - ColBERT token vectors are stored in LMDB for reranking.

(match #30, match line: 109, block lines: 84-134, block type: context, matched by: muvera)

    84: Rationale: Clear top-level data location, separate from app code.
    85: Alternatives:
    86: - `backend/app/lmdb/`
    87:   - Pros: Co-locates with application code.
    88:   - Cons: Mixes data with code.
    89: 
    90: ## Retrieval Workflow (Target)
    91: 1) Download and chunk
    92: 2) Embed with ColBERT
    93: 3) Weaviate applies MUVERA encoding to multi-vector field at index time
    94: 4) Retrieve Weaviate UUIDs and load ColBERT vectors from LMDB by UUID
    95: # MUVERA multi-vector indexing (Weaviate)
    96: Weaviate’s MUVERA expects **multi-vector** inputs and encodes them internally into fixed-length vectors for indexing.
    97: 
    98: Doc-aligned config (local client):
    99: 
   100: ```python
   101: from weaviate.classes.config import Configure
   102: 
   103: client.collections.create(
   104:     name="PatentData",
   105:     properties=[...],
   106:     vector_config=[
   107:         Configure.MultiVectors.self_provided(
   108:             name="colbert",
   109:             encoding=Configure.VectorIndex.MultiVector.Encoding.muvera(),
   110:         )
   111:     ],
   112: )
   113: ```
   114: 
   115: Insert usage:
   116: 
   117: ```python
   118: collection.data.insert(
   119:     properties={...},
   120:     vector={"colbert": multi_vector_colbert},
   121: )
   122: ```
   123: 
   124: Notes:
   125: - You must send **multi‑vector ColBERT** at ingest **and** query time.
   126: - MUVERA encodes internally; you don’t store a separate “muvera” vector.
   127: 
   128: Weaviate batching
   129: - `store.py` uses `collection.data.insert_many` to batch inserts.
   130: - Batch size is controlled by `WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE` (default 128).
   131: 
   132: LMDB ColBERT storage
   133: - ColBERT token vectors are stored in LMDB for reranking.
   134: - Stored as FP16 by default (`LMDB_VECTOR_DTYPE=float16`) to save ~2× space.

===== FILE: DESIGN_CHOICES.md =====
(match #31, match line: 120, block lines: 95-135, block type: context, matched by: multi_vector, colbert)

    95: # MUVERA multi-vector indexing (Weaviate)
    96: Weaviate’s MUVERA expects **multi-vector** inputs and encodes them internally into fixed-length vectors for indexing.
    97: 
    98: Doc-aligned config (local client):
    99: 
   100: ```python
   101: from weaviate.classes.config import Configure
   102: 
   103: client.collections.create(
   104:     name="PatentData",
   105:     properties=[...],
   106:     vector_config=[
   107:         Configure.MultiVectors.self_provided(
   108:             name="colbert",
   109:             encoding=Configure.VectorIndex.MultiVector.Encoding.muvera(),
   110:         )
   111:     ],
   112: )
   113: ```
   114: 
   115: Insert usage:
   116: 
   117: ```python
   118: collection.data.insert(
   119:     properties={...},
   120:     vector={"colbert": multi_vector_colbert},
   121: )
   122: ```
   123: 
   124: Notes:
   125: - You must send **multi‑vector ColBERT** at ingest **and** query time.
   126: - MUVERA encodes internally; you don’t store a separate “muvera” vector.
   127: 
   128: Weaviate batching
   129: - `store.py` uses `collection.data.insert_many` to batch inserts.
   130: - Batch size is controlled by `WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE` (default 128).
   131: 
   132: LMDB ColBERT storage
   133: - ColBERT token vectors are stored in LMDB for reranking.
   134: - Stored as FP16 by default (`LMDB_VECTOR_DTYPE=float16`) to save ~2× space.
   135: - Map size grows automatically on `MDB_MAP_FULL` by `LMDB_MAP_GROW_GB` (default 10 GB).

===== FILE: DESIGN_CHOICES.md =====

   100: ```python
   101: from weaviate.classes.config import Configure
   102: 
   103: client.collections.create(

   106:     vector_config=[
   107:         Configure.MultiVectors.self_provided(

   121: )
   122: ```
   123: 
   124: Notes:
   125: - You must send **multi‑vector ColBERT** at ingest **and** query time.
   126: - MUVERA encodes internally; you don’t store a separate “muvera” vector.

   129: - `store.py` uses `collection.data.insert_many` to batch inserts.
   130: - Batch size is controlled by `WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE` (default 128).
   131:

   133: - ColBERT token vectors are stored in LMDB for reranking.

   120:     vector={"colbert": multi_vector_colbert},
   121: )
   122: ```
   123: 
   124: Notes:
   125: - You must send **multi‑vector ColBERT** at ingest **and** query time.
   126: - MUVERA encodes internally; you don’t store a separate “muvera” vector.
   127: 
   128: Weaviate batching
   129: - `store.py` uses `collection.data.insert_many` to batch inserts.
   130: - Batch size is controlled by `WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE` (default 128).
   131: 
   132: LMDB ColBERT storage
   133: - ColBERT token vectors are stored in LMDB for reranking.
   134: - Stored as FP16 by default (`LMDB_VECTOR_DTYPE=float16`) to save ~2× space.
   135: - Map size grows automatically on `MDB_MAP_FULL` by `LMDB_MAP_GROW_GB` (default 10 GB).

   267: def _maxsim_score(query_tokens: np.ndarray, doc_tokens: np.ndarray) -> float:
   268:     if query_tokens.size == 0 or doc_tokens.size == 0:
   269:         return float("-inf")
   270:     qt = query_tokens.astype(np.float32, copy=False)
   271:     dt = doc_tokens.astype(np.float32, copy=False)
   272:     if qt.ndim != 2 or dt.ndim != 2:
   273:         return float("-inf")
   274:     if qt.shape[1] != dt.shape[1]:
   275:         return float("-inf")
   276:     sims = qt @ dt.T
   277:     return float(np.max(sims, axis=1).sum())
   278: 
   279: 
   280: def _rerank_hits_with_lmdb(hits: List[Dict[str, Any]], query: str, shard: str, rerank_k: int) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   281:     shard = assert_128_variant(shard, context="orchestrator._rerank_hits_with_lmdb")
   282:     if shard not in SHARD_TO_PATH:
   283:         raise ValueError(f"Unknown shard '{shard}'. Choose from: {sorted(SHARD_TO_PATH)}")
   284:     q_tokens = _embed_query_tokens_for_shard(query, shard)
   285:     top = hits[: max(0, int(rerank_k))]
   286:     scored = []
   287:     for hit in top:
   288:         addl = hit.get("_additional") or {}
   289:         obj_id = addl.get("id")
   290:         claim_id = hit.get("claim_id")
   291:         doc_id = hit.get("doc_id")
   292:         if not obj_id and not claim_id:
   293:             continue
   294:         lmdb_path = resolve_lmdb_path(shard, doc_id=doc_id)
   295:         # Primary LMDB key is claim_id; keep UUID fallback for older ingests.
   296:         lookup_keys = [k for k in [claim_id, obj_id] if k]
   297:         doc_tokens = None
   298:         for k in lookup_keys:
   299:             doc_tokens = load_colbert_from_lmdb(lmdb_path, str(k))
   300:             if doc_tokens is not None:
   301:                 break
   302:         if doc_tokens is None:
   303:             raise RuntimeError(f"Missing LMDB vectors for claim_id={claim_id} doc_id={doc_id}")
   304:         arr = np.asarray(doc_tokens)
   305:         if arr.ndim == 1:
   306:             arr = arr.reshape(1, -1)
   307:         if arr.ndim != 2 or arr.shape[1] != 128:
   308:             raise ValueError(
   309:                 f"LMDB vectors must be [T,128] for claim_id={claim_id}; got shape={arr.shape}"
   310:             )
   311:         score = _maxsim_score(q_tokens, doc_tokens)
   312:         if score == float("-inf"):
   313:             raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid MaxSim score for claim_id={claim_id}")
   314:         scored.append((score, hit))
   315:     scored.sort(key=lambda x: x[0], reverse=True)
   316:     reranked = [hit for _, hit in scored]
   317:     used = {id(hit) for hit in reranked}
   318:     for hit in top:
   319:         if id(hit) not in used:
   320:             reranked.append(hit)
   321:     reranked.extend(hits[int(rerank_k):])
   322:     return reranked
   323: 
   324: 
   325: @tool(response_format="content")
   326: def retrieve_context(
   327:     query: str,
   328:     where_filter: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
   329:     retrieval_mode: Optional[str] = None,
   330:     hybrid_alpha: Optional[float] = None,
   331: ):
   332:     """Retrieve information to help answer a query, optionally using metadata filters.
   333: 
   334:     Args:
   335:         query: Search terms to look for
   336:         where_filter: Filter for database search
   337:     """
   338:     if not query or not query.strip():
   339:         return {"query": query, "joined_text": "", "chunks": []}
   340: 
   341:     query_text = query.strip()
   342:     effective_mode = _normalize_retrieval_mode(retrieval_mode)

   324: 
   325: @tool(response_format="content")
   326: def retrieve_context(
   327:     query: str,
   328:     where_filter: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
   329:     retrieval_mode: Optional[str] = None,
   330:     hybrid_alpha: Optional[float] = None,
   331: ):
   332:     """Retrieve information to help answer a query, optionally using metadata filters.
   333: 
   334:     Args:
   335:         query: Search terms to look for
   336:         where_filter: Filter for database search
   337:     """
   338:     if not query or not query.strip():
   339:         return {"query": query, "joined_text": "", "chunks": []}
   340: 
   341:     query_text = query.strip()
   342:     effective_mode = _normalize_retrieval_mode(retrieval_mode)
   343:     effective_alpha = HYBRID_ALPHA if hybrid_alpha is None else max(0.0, min(1.0, float(hybrid_alpha)))
   344:     query_vector = _embed_query_colbert(query_text) if effective_mode in {"vector", "hybrid"} else []
   345:     retrieval_clause = _build_retrieval_clause(query_text, effective_mode, effective_alpha, query_vector)
   346:     doc_id_filters = _extract_doc_id_filters(where_filter)
   347:     claim_where = _build_claim_where(doc_id_filters)
   348:     if effective_mode == "hybrid" and 0.0 < effective_alpha < 1.0 and FORCE_CLIENT_HYBRID:
   349:         hits = _retrieve_hybrid_client_fusion(
   350:             query_text,
   351:             claim_where,
   352:             query_vector,
   353:             effective_alpha,
   354:             int(DEFAULT_CANDIDATE_LIMIT),
   355:         )
   356:     else:
   357:         try:
   358:             hits = _query_claim_rows(retrieval_clause, claim_where, int(DEFAULT_CANDIDATE_LIMIT))
   359:         except Exception as e:
   360:             if effective_mode != "hybrid" or effective_alpha <= 0.0 or effective_alpha >= 1.0:
   361:                 raise
   362:             print(f"[warn] server-side hybrid failed; using client-side fusion fallback: {e}")
   363:             hits = _retrieve_hybrid_client_fusion(
   364:                 query_text,
   365:                 claim_where,
   366:                 query_vector,
   367:                 effective_alpha,
   368:                 int(DEFAULT_CANDIDATE_LIMIT),
   369:             )
   370: 
   371:     hits = _rerank_hits_with_lmdb(hits, query.strip(), RERANK_SHARD, RERANK_K)
   372:     hits = hits[:DEFAULT_LIMIT]
   373: 
   374:     claim_payloads = load_claim_payloads_from_lmdb(
   375:         [str(hit.get("claim_id", "")).strip() for hit in hits if str(hit.get("claim_id", "")).strip()]
   376:     )
   377: 
   378:     unique_doc_ids = []
   379:     seen_doc_ids = set()
   380:     for hit in hits:
   381:         claim_id = str(hit.get("claim_id", "")).strip()
   382:         payload = claim_payloads.get(claim_id) or {}
   383:         did = str(hit.get("doc_id", "") or payload.get("doc_id") or "").strip()
   384:         if did and did not in seen_doc_ids:
   385:             seen_doc_ids.add(did)
   386:             unique_doc_ids.append(did)
   387: 
   388:     patent_meta = _fetch_patent_metadata(unique_doc_ids)
   389: 
   390:     chunks: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   391:     for hit in hits:
   392:         addl = hit.get("_additional") or {}
   393:         claim_id = str(hit.get("claim_id", "")).strip()
   394:         payload = claim_payloads.get(claim_id) or {}
   395:         doc_id = str(hit.get("doc_id", "") or payload.get("doc_id") or "").strip()
   396:         patent_row = patent_meta.get(doc_id, {})
   397:         text = rich_to_plain(str(payload.get("text") or ""))
   398:         snippet = text[:500]
   399: 
   400:         # Include SearchItem-compatible fields in metadata for API shaping.
   401:         metadata = {
   402:             "id": hit.get("claim_id") or addl.get("id", ""),
   403:             "title": patent_row.get("title", ""),
   404:             "snippet": snippet,
   405:             "search_text": text,
   406:             "doc_id": doc_id,
   407:             "claim_id": claim_id,
   408:             "claim_type": str(payload.get("claim_type") or ""),
   409:             "distance": addl.get("distance"),
   410:             "filing_date": patent_row.get("filing_date", ""),
   411:             "classification": patent_row.get("classification", ""),
   412:             "authors": patent_row.get("authors", []),
   413:             "kind": patent_row.get("kind", ""),
   414:         }
   415:         chunks.append({"text": text, "metadata": metadata})
   416: 
   417:     joined_text = "\n\n".join(c["text"] for c in chunks if c.get("text"))
   418:     return {
   419:         "query": query,
   420:         "query_vector": query_vector,
   421:         "retrieval_mode": effective_mode,
   422:         "hybrid_alpha": effective_alpha,
   423:         "joined_text": joined_text,
   424:         "chunks": chunks,
   425:     }
   426:

   428: retriever_tool = retrieve_context

   190: def _project_tokens_128(masked_token_embeddings: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   191:     if masked_token_embeddings.ndim != 2 or masked_token_embeddings.shape[1] != 768:
   192:         raise ValueError(
   193:             f"Expected token embeddings shape [T,768] before projection. "
   194:             f"Got {tuple(masked_token_embeddings.shape)}."
   195:         )
   196:     proj = _load_trained_projection_matrix()
   197:     tokens = masked_token_embeddings.to(device=DEVICE, dtype=proj.dtype)
   198:     projected = tokens @ proj.transpose(0, 1)
   199:     if projected.ndim != 2 or projected.shape[1] != 128:
   200:         raise ValueError(
   201:             f"Projected token embeddings must have shape [T,128]. Got {tuple(projected.shape)}."
   202:         )
   203:     return F.normalize(projected.to(torch.float32), p=2, dim=1)
   204: 
   205: 
   206: def _build_token_variants(projected_token_vectors: torch.Tensor) -> dict[str, object]:
   207:     if projected_token_vectors.ndim != 2 or projected_token_vectors.shape[1] != 128:
   208:         raise ValueError(
   209:             f"Token vectors must be [T,128] before variant export. "
   210:             f"Got {tuple(projected_token_vectors.shape)}."
   211:         )
   212:     variants: dict[str, object] = {}
   213:     if "128_f32" in ENABLED_COLBERT_VARIANTS:
   214:         variants["128_f32"] = projected_token_vectors.to(torch.float32).detach().cpu().numpy()
   215:     if "128_f16" in ENABLED_COLBERT_VARIANTS:
   216:         variants["128_f16"] = projected_token_vectors.to(torch.float16).detach().cpu().numpy()
   217:     if not variants:
   218:         raise ValueError(
   219:             f"No enabled 128-d variants found in configuration: {sorted(ENABLED_COLBERT_VARIANTS)}"
   220:         )
   221:     return variants
   222: 
   223: 
   224: def normalize_text_for_embedding(text: str | None) -> str:
   225:     """
   226:     Shared normalization for all text that is sent to the embedding model.
   227:     Keeping claims and queries on the same normalization path avoids drift.
   228:     """
   229:     return rich_to_plain((text or "").strip())
   230:

   233:     if not text:

   213:     if "128_f32" in ENABLED_COLBERT_VARIANTS:
   214:         variants["128_f32"] = projected_token_vectors.to(torch.float32).detach().cpu().numpy()
   215:     if "128_f16" in ENABLED_COLBERT_VARIANTS:
   216:         variants["128_f16"] = projected_token_vectors.to(torch.float16).detach().cpu().numpy()
   217:     if not variants:
   218:         raise ValueError(
   219:             f"No enabled 128-d variants found in configuration: {sorted(ENABLED_COLBERT_VARIANTS)}"
   220:         )
   221:     return variants
   222: 
   223: 
   224: def normalize_text_for_embedding(text: str | None) -> str:
   225:     """
   226:     Shared normalization for all text that is sent to the embedding model.
   227:     Keeping claims and queries on the same normalization path avoids drift.
   228:     """
   229:     return rich_to_plain((text or "").strip())
   230: 
   231: 
   232: def _token_count(text: str, tokenizer) -> int:
   233:     if not text:
   234:         return 0
   235:     with warnings.catch_warnings():
   236:         warnings.filterwarnings(
   237:             "ignore",
   238:             message="Token indices sequence length is longer than the specified maximum sequence length.*",
   239:         )
   240:         return tokenizer(text, return_tensors="pt", truncation=False)["input_ids"].shape[1]
   241: 

===== FILE: backend/app/embed.py =====
(match #62, match line: 339, block lines: 316-447, block type: function+context, function: embed_chunks, matched by: colbert)

   316:     expanded = []
   317:     for ch in chunks:
   318:         text = normalize_text_for_embedding(ch.get("text") or ch.get("chunk"))
   319:         if not text:
   320:             expanded.append(ch)
   321:             continue
   322:         token_count = _token_count(text, tokenizer)
   323:         if token_count <= max_length:
   324:             expanded.append(ch)
   325:             continue
   326:         parts = _split_oversize_text(text, tokenizer, max_length, overlap_tokens)
   327:         part_count = len(parts)
   328:         for idx, part in enumerate(parts, start=1):
   329:             new_chunk = dict(ch)
   330:             new_chunk["chunk"] = part
   331:             new_chunk["text"] = part
   332:             new_chunk["part_index"] = idx
   333:             new_chunk["part_count"] = part_count
   334:             expanded.append(new_chunk)
   335:     return expanded
   336: def embed_chunks(chunks, tokenizer, model, max_length=350):
   337:     print("Starting embedding process...")
   338:     print(f"[embed-config] {_projection_summary()}")
   339: # Our list of embeddings, and runs ColBERT in evaluation mode. Model normally runs in train()   #
   340: # mode, which will drop embeddings and normalize to prevent overfitting.                        #
   341:     embeddings = []
   342:     model.eval()
   343: 
   344:     model_max = getattr(tokenizer, "model_max_length", None)
   345:     effective_max = max_length
   346:     if model_max and isinstance(model_max, int):
   347:         effective_max = min(max_length, model_max)
   348: 
   349:     chunks = _expand_chunks_for_token_limit(
   350:         chunks,
   351:         tokenizer,
   352:         effective_max,
   353:         overlap_tokens=REBALANCE_OVERLAP_TOKENS,
   354:     )
   355: 
   356: # Loop through the chunks based on label, clean text up further, and then tokenize.             #
   357:     progress_every = int(os.environ.get("EMBED_PROGRESS_EVERY", "50"))
   358:     for idx, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
   359:         if progress_every > 0 and (idx == 0 or (idx + 1) % progress_every == 0):
   360:             print(f"Embedding chunk {idx + 1} of {len(chunks)})")
   361:         text = normalize_text_for_embedding(chunk.get("text") or chunk.get("chunk"))
   362: 
   363:         if not text:
   364:             print(f"Empty chunk at index {idx}")
   365:             continue
   366: 
   367: # Call the tokenizer function.                                                                  #
   368:         tokens = tokenizer(
   369:             text,                        # chunk text
   370:             return_tensors="pt",        # change format from lists to pt tensors so ColBERT can read
   371:             truncation=True,            # truncate chunks that go over the token limits
   372:             padding="max_length",       # add zero tokens to make sure tensors are equal size. Helps the model embed faster
   373:             max_length=effective_max     # the token limit
   374:         )
   375:         tokens = {k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in tokens.items()}
   376: 
   377: # Warning message if a chunk was truncated, this means I need to make chunks shorter.           #
   378:         if tokens["input_ids"].shape[1] == effective_max:
   379:             token_count = _token_count(text, tokenizer)
   380:             if token_count > effective_max:
   381:                 print(
   382:                     f"WARNING chunk {chunk.get('section')} truncated from "
   383:                     f"{token_count} tokens to {effective_max}."
   384:                 )
   385: 
   386: # Embed the tokenized text by running it through ColBERT. Tell Pytorch not to track gradients.  #
   387: # stops the collection of tracking data to save a lot of computing time.                        #
   388: # At this point, we now have a set of embeddings for a chunk.                                   #
   389:         with torch.no_grad():
   390:             outputs = model(**tokens)
   391:             token_embeddings = outputs.last_hidden_state.squeeze(0)
   392:             if token_embeddings.shape[-1] != 768:
   393:                 raise ValueError(
   394:                     f"Expected 768-d token embeddings, got {token_embeddings.shape[-1]}"
   395:                 )
   396:             attn_mask = tokens["attention_mask"].squeeze(0).bool()
   397:             masked_embeddings = token_embeddings[attn_mask]
   398:             if masked_embeddings.numel() == 0:
   399:                 raise ValueError("No non-padding tokens available after attention_mask filtering.")
   400:             projected_tokens = _project_tokens_128(masked_embeddings)
   401:             if projected_tokens.shape[-1] != 128:
   402:                 raise ValueError(
   403:                     f"Projected token embeddings must be 128-d. Got {projected_tokens.shape[-1]}."
   404:                 )
   405:             chunk_vector = (
   406:                 projected_tokens.mean(dim=0)
   407:                 .detach()
   408:                 .cpu()
   409:                 .numpy()
   410:                 .tolist()
   411:             )
   412:             colbert_variants = _build_token_variants(projected_tokens)
   413:             colbert_vectors = colbert_variants.get(PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT)
   414:             if colbert_vectors is None:
   415:                 raise ValueError(
   416:                     f"Configured primary ColBERT variant '{PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT}' is unavailable. "
   417:                     f"Available={sorted(colbert_variants.keys())}"
   418:                 )
   419:             if np.asarray(colbert_vectors).shape[-1] != 128:
   420:                 raise ValueError("Configured ColBERT vectors are not 128-d.")
   421: 
   422:         chunk["embedding"] = chunk_vector
   423:         chunk["colbert"] = colbert_vectors
   424:         chunk["colbert_variants"] = colbert_variants
   425:         embeddings.append(chunk)
   426: 
   427:     return embeddings
   428: 
   429: def embed_query(query, tokenizer, model, max_length=256):
   430:     model.eval()
   431:     query_text = normalize_text_for_embedding(query)
   432:  
   433:     tokens = tokenizer(
   434:         query_text,
   435:         return_tensors="pt", 
   436:         truncation=True, 
   437:         padding="max_length", 
   438:         max_length=max_length
   439:         )
   440:     tokens = {k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in tokens.items()}
   441:     
   442:     with torch.no_grad():
   443:         outputs = model(**tokens)
   444: 
   445:     token_embeddings = outputs.last_hidden_state.squeeze(0)
   446:     if token_embeddings.shape[-1] != 768:
   447:         raise ValueError(f"Expected query token embeddings [T,768], got {tuple(token_embeddings.shape)}.")

   387: # stops the collection of tracking data to save a lot of computing time.                        #
   388: # At this point, we now have a set of embeddings for a chunk.                                   #
   389:         with torch.no_grad():
   390:             outputs = model(**tokens)
   391:             token_embeddings = outputs.last_hidden_state.squeeze(0)
   392:             if token_embeddings.shape[-1] != 768:
   393:                 raise ValueError(
   394:                     f"Expected 768-d token embeddings, got {token_embeddings.shape[-1]}"
   395:                 )
   396:             attn_mask = tokens["attention_mask"].squeeze(0).bool()
   397:             masked_embeddings = token_embeddings[attn_mask]
   398:             if masked_embeddings.numel() == 0:
   399:                 raise ValueError("No non-padding tokens available after attention_mask filtering.")
   400:             projected_tokens = _project_tokens_128(masked_embeddings)
   401:             if projected_tokens.shape[-1] != 128:
   402:                 raise ValueError(
   403:                     f"Projected token embeddings must be 128-d. Got {projected_tokens.shape[-1]}."
   404:                 )
   405:             chunk_vector = (
   406:                 projected_tokens.mean(dim=0)
   407:                 .detach()
   408:                 .cpu()
   409:                 .numpy()
   410:                 .tolist()
   411:             )
   412:             colbert_variants = _build_token_variants(projected_tokens)
   413:             colbert_vectors = colbert_variants.get(PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT)
   414:             if colbert_vectors is None:
   415:                 raise ValueError(
   416:                     f"Configured primary ColBERT variant '{PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT}' is unavailable. "
   417:                     f"Available={sorted(colbert_variants.keys())}"
   418:                 )
   419:             if np.asarray(colbert_vectors).shape[-1] != 128:
   420:                 raise ValueError("Configured ColBERT vectors are not 128-d.")
   421: 
   422:         chunk["embedding"] = chunk_vector
   423:         chunk["colbert"] = colbert_vectors
   424:         chunk["colbert_variants"] = colbert_variants
   425:         embeddings.append(chunk)
   426: 
   427:     return embeddings
   428: 
   429: def embed_query(query, tokenizer, model, max_length=256):
   430:     model.eval()
   431:     query_text = normalize_text_for_embedding(query)
   432:  
   433:     tokens = tokenizer(
   434:         query_text,
   435:         return_tensors="pt", 
   436:         truncation=True, 
   437:         padding="max_length", 
   438:         max_length=max_length
   439:         )
   440:     tokens = {k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in tokens.items()}
   441:     
   442:     with torch.no_grad():
   443:         outputs = model(**tokens)
   444: 
   445:     token_embeddings = outputs.last_hidden_state.squeeze(0)
   446:     if token_embeddings.shape[-1] != 768:
   447:         raise ValueError(f"Expected query token embeddings [T,768], got {tuple(token_embeddings.shape)}.")

   316:     expanded = []
   317:     for ch in chunks:
   318:         text = normalize_text_for_embedding(ch.get("text") or ch.get("chunk"))
   319:         if not text:
   320:             expanded.append(ch)

   322:         token_count = _token_count(text, tokenizer)
   323:         if token_count <= max_length:
   324:             expanded.append(ch)
   325:             continue
   326:         parts = _split_oversize_text(text, tokenizer, max_length, overlap_tokens)
   327:         part_count = len(parts)
   328:         for idx, part in enumerate(parts, start=1):
   329:             new_chunk = dict(ch)
   330:             new_chunk["chunk"] = part
   331:             new_chunk["text"] = part
   332:             new_chunk["part_index"] = idx
   333:             new_chunk["part_count"] = part_count
   334:             expanded.append(new_chunk)
   335:     return expanded
   336: def embed_chunks(chunks, tokenizer, model, max_length=350):
   337:     print("Starting embedding process...")
   338:     print(f"[embed-config] {_projection_summary()}")
   339: # Our list of embeddings, and runs ColBERT in evaluation mode. Model normally runs in train()   #
   340: # mode, which will drop embeddings and normalize to prevent overfitting.                        #
   341:     embeddings = []
   342:     model.eval()
   343: 
   344:     model_max = getattr(tokenizer, "model_max_length", None)
   345:     effective_max = max_length
   346:     if model_max and isinstance(model_max, int):
   347:         effective_max = min(max_length, model_max)
   348: 
   349:     chunks = _expand_chunks_for_token_limit(
   350:         chunks,
   351:         tokenizer,
   352:         effective_max,
   353:         overlap_tokens=REBALANCE_OVERLAP_TOKENS,
   354:     )
   355: 
   356: # Loop through the chunks based on label, clean text up further, and then tokenize.             #
   357:     progress_every = int(os.environ.get("EMBED_PROGRESS_EVERY", "50"))
   358:     for idx, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
   359:         if progress_every > 0 and (idx == 0 or (idx + 1) % progress_every == 0):
   360:             print(f"Embedding chunk {idx + 1} of {len(chunks)})")
   361:         text = normalize_text_for_embedding(chunk.get("text") or chunk.get("chunk"))
   362: 
   363:         if not text:
   364:             print(f"Empty chunk at index {idx}")
   365:             continue
   366: 
   367: # Call the tokenizer function.                                                                  #
   368:         tokens = tokenizer(
   369:             text,                        # chunk text
   370:             return_tensors="pt",        # change format from lists to pt tensors so ColBERT can read
   371:             truncation=True,            # truncate chunks that go over the token limits
   372:             padding="max_length",       # add zero tokens to make sure tensors are equal size. Helps the model embed faster
   373:             max_length=effective_max     # the token limit
   374:         )
   375:         tokens = {k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in tokens.items()}
   376: 
   377: # Warning message if a chunk was truncated, this means I need to make chunks shorter.           #
   378:         if tokens["input_ids"].shape[1] == effective_max:
   379:             token_count = _token_count(text, tokenizer)
   380:             if token_count > effective_max:
   381:                 print(
   382:                     f"WARNING chunk {chunk.get('section')} truncated from "
   383:                     f"{token_count} tokens to {effective_max}."
   384:                 )
   385: 
   386: # Embed the tokenized text by running it through ColBERT. Tell Pytorch not to track gradients.  #
   387: # stops the collection of tracking data to save a lot of computing time.                        #
   388: # At this point, we now have a set of embeddings for a chunk.                                   #
   389:         with torch.no_grad():
   390:             outputs = model(**tokens)
   391:             token_embeddings = outputs.last_hidden_state.squeeze(0)
   392:             if token_embeddings.shape[-1] != 768:
   393:                 raise ValueError(
   394:                     f"Expected 768-d token embeddings, got {token_embeddings.shape[-1]}"
   395:                 )
   396:             attn_mask = tokens["attention_mask"].squeeze(0).bool()
   397:             masked_embeddings = token_embeddings[attn_mask]
   398:             if masked_embeddings.numel() == 0:
   399:                 raise ValueError("No non-padding tokens available after attention_mask filtering.")
   400:             projected_tokens = _project_tokens_128(masked_embeddings)
   401:             if projected_tokens.shape[-1] != 128:
   402:                 raise ValueError(
   403:                     f"Projected token embeddings must be 128-d. Got {projected_tokens.shape[-1]}."
   404:                 )
   405:             chunk_vector = (
   406:                 projected_tokens.mean(dim=0)
   407:                 .detach()
   408:                 .cpu()
   409:                 .numpy()
   410:                 .tolist()
   411:             )
   412:             colbert_variants = _build_token_variants(projected_tokens)
   413:             colbert_vectors = colbert_variants.get(PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT)
   414:             if colbert_vectors is None:
   415:                 raise ValueError(
   416:                     f"Configured primary ColBERT variant '{PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT}' is unavailable. "
   417:                     f"Available={sorted(colbert_variants.keys())}"
   418:                 )
   419:             if np.asarray(colbert_vectors).shape[-1] != 128:
   420:                 raise ValueError("Configured ColBERT vectors are not 128-d.")
   421: 
   422:         chunk["embedding"] = chunk_vector
   423:         chunk["colbert"] = colbert_vectors
   424:         chunk["colbert_variants"] = colbert_variants
   425:         embeddings.append(chunk)
   426: 
   427:     return embeddings
   428:

   357:     progress_every = int(os.environ.get("EMBED_PROGRESS_EVERY", "50"))
   358:     for idx, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
   359:         if progress_every > 0 and (idx == 0 or (idx + 1) % progress_every == 0):
   360:             print(f"Embedding chunk {idx + 1} of {len(chunks)})")
   361:         text = normalize_text_for_embedding(chunk.get("text") or chunk.get("chunk"))
   362: 
   363:         if not text:
   364:             print(f"Empty chunk at index {idx}")
   365:             continue
   366: 
   367: # Call the tokenizer function.                                                                  #
   368:         tokens = tokenizer(
   369:             text,                        # chunk text
   370:             return_tensors="pt",        # change format from lists to pt tensors so ColBERT can read
   371:             truncation=True,            # truncate chunks that go over the token limits
   372:             padding="max_length",       # add zero tokens to make sure tensors are equal size. Helps the model embed faster
   373:             max_length=effective_max     # the token limit
   374:         )
   375:         tokens = {k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in tokens.items()}
   376: 
   377: # Warning message if a chunk was truncated, this means I need to make chunks shorter.           #
   378:         if tokens["input_ids"].shape[1] == effective_max:
   379:             token_count = _token_count(text, tokenizer)
   380:             if token_count > effective_max:
   381:                 print(
   382:                     f"WARNING chunk {chunk.get('section')} truncated from "
   383:                     f"{token_count} tokens to {effective_max}."
   384:                 )
   385: 
   386: # Embed the tokenized text by running it through ColBERT. Tell Pytorch not to track gradients.  #
   387: # stops the collection of tracking data to save a lot of computing time.                        #
   388: # At this point, we now have a set of embeddings for a chunk.                                   #
   389:         with torch.no_grad():
   390:             outputs = model(**tokens)
   391:             token_embeddings = outputs.last_hidden_state.squeeze(0)
   392:             if token_embeddings.shape[-1] != 768:
   393:                 raise ValueError(
   394:                     f"Expected 768-d token embeddings, got {token_embeddings.shape[-1]}"
   395:                 )
   396:             attn_mask = tokens["attention_mask"].squeeze(0).bool()
   397:             masked_embeddings = token_embeddings[attn_mask]
   398:             if masked_embeddings.numel() == 0:
   399:                 raise ValueError("No non-padding tokens available after attention_mask filtering.")
   400:             projected_tokens = _project_tokens_128(masked_embeddings)
   401:             if projected_tokens.shape[-1] != 128:
   402:                 raise ValueError(
   403:                     f"Projected token embeddings must be 128-d. Got {projected_tokens.shape[-1]}."
   404:                 )
   405:             chunk_vector = (
   406:                 projected_tokens.mean(dim=0)
   407:                 .detach()
   408:                 .cpu()
   409:                 .numpy()
   410:                 .tolist()
   411:             )
   412:             colbert_variants = _build_token_variants(projected_tokens)
   413:             colbert_vectors = colbert_variants.get(PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT)
   414:             if colbert_vectors is None:
   415:                 raise ValueError(
   416:                     f"Configured primary ColBERT variant '{PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT}' is unavailable. "
   417:                     f"Available={sorted(colbert_variants.keys())}"
   418:                 )
   419:             if np.asarray(colbert_vectors).shape[-1] != 128:
   420:                 raise ValueError("Configured ColBERT vectors are not 128-d.")
   421: 
   422:         chunk["embedding"] = chunk_vector
   423:         chunk["colbert"] = colbert_vectors
   424:         chunk["colbert_variants"] = colbert_variants
   425:         embeddings.append(chunk)
   426: 
   427:     return embeddings
   428: 
   429: def embed_query(query, tokenizer, model, max_length=256):
   430:     model.eval()
   431:     query_text = normalize_text_for_embedding(query)
   432:  
   433:     tokens = tokenizer(
   434:         query_text,
   435:         return_tensors="pt", 
   436:         truncation=True, 
   437:         padding="max_length", 
   438:         max_length=max_length
   439:         )
   440:     tokens = {k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in tokens.items()}
   441:     
   442:     with torch.no_grad():
   443:         outputs = model(**tokens)
   444: 
   445:     token_embeddings = outputs.last_hidden_state.squeeze(0)
   446:     if token_embeddings.shape[-1] != 768:
   447:         raise ValueError(f"Expected query token embeddings [T,768], got {tuple(token_embeddings.shape)}.")

   329:             new_chunk = dict(ch)

   331:             new_chunk["text"] = part
   332:             new_chunk["part_index"] = idx
   333:             new_chunk["part_count"] = part_count

   336: def embed_chunks(chunks, tokenizer, model, max_length=350):
   337:     print("Starting embedding process...")
   338:     print(f"[embed-config] {_projection_summary()}")
   339: # Our list of embeddings, and runs ColBERT in evaluation mode. Model normally runs in train()   #
   340: # mode, which will drop embeddings and normalize to prevent overfitting.                        #
   341:     embeddings = []
   342:     model.eval()
   343: 
   344:     model_max = getattr(tokenizer, "model_max_length", None)
   345:     effective_max = max_length
   346:     if model_max and isinstance(model_max, int):
   347:         effective_max = min(max_length, model_max)
   348: 
   349:     chunks = _expand_chunks_for_token_limit(
   350:         chunks,
   351:         tokenizer,
   352:         effective_max,
   353:         overlap_tokens=REBALANCE_OVERLAP_TOKENS,
   354:     )
   355: 
   356: # Loop through the chunks based on label, clean text up further, and then tokenize.             #
   357:     progress_every = int(os.environ.get("EMBED_PROGRESS_EVERY", "50"))
   358:     for idx, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
   359:         if progress_every > 0 and (idx == 0 or (idx + 1) % progress_every == 0):
   360:             print(f"Embedding chunk {idx + 1} of {len(chunks)})")
   361:         text = normalize_text_for_embedding(chunk.get("text") or chunk.get("chunk"))
   362: 
   363:         if not text:
   364:             print(f"Empty chunk at index {idx}")
   365:             continue
   366: 
   367: # Call the tokenizer function.                                                                  #
   368:         tokens = tokenizer(
   369:             text,                        # chunk text
   370:             return_tensors="pt",        # change format from lists to pt tensors so ColBERT can read
   371:             truncation=True,            # truncate chunks that go over the token limits
   372:             padding="max_length",       # add zero tokens to make sure tensors are equal size. Helps the model embed faster
   373:             max_length=effective_max     # the token limit
   374:         )
   375:         tokens = {k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in tokens.items()}
   376: 
   377: # Warning message if a chunk was truncated, this means I need to make chunks shorter.           #
   378:         if tokens["input_ids"].shape[1] == effective_max:
   379:             token_count = _token_count(text, tokenizer)
   380:             if token_count > effective_max:
   381:                 print(
   382:                     f"WARNING chunk {chunk.get('section')} truncated from "
   383:                     f"{token_count} tokens to {effective_max}."
   384:                 )
   385: 
   386: # Embed the tokenized text by running it through ColBERT. Tell Pytorch not to track gradients.  #
   387: # stops the collection of tracking data to save a lot of computing time.                        #
   388: # At this point, we now have a set of embeddings for a chunk.                                   #
   389:         with torch.no_grad():
   390:             outputs = model(**tokens)
   391:             token_embeddings = outputs.last_hidden_state.squeeze(0)
   392:             if token_embeddings.shape[-1] != 768:
   393:                 raise ValueError(
   394:                     f"Expected 768-d token embeddings, got {token_embeddings.shape[-1]}"
   395:                 )
   396:             attn_mask = tokens["attention_mask"].squeeze(0).bool()
   397:             masked_embeddings = token_embeddings[attn_mask]
   398:             if masked_embeddings.numel() == 0:
   399:                 raise ValueError("No non-padding tokens available after attention_mask filtering.")
   400:             projected_tokens = _project_tokens_128(masked_embeddings)
   401:             if projected_tokens.shape[-1] != 128:
   402:                 raise ValueError(
   403:                     f"Projected token embeddings must be 128-d. Got {projected_tokens.shape[-1]}."
   404:                 )
   405:             chunk_vector = (
   406:                 projected_tokens.mean(dim=0)
   407:                 .detach()
   408:                 .cpu()
   409:                 .numpy()
   410:                 .tolist()
   411:             )
   412:             colbert_variants = _build_token_variants(projected_tokens)
   413:             colbert_vectors = colbert_variants.get(PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT)
   414:             if colbert_vectors is None:
   415:                 raise ValueError(
   416:                     f"Configured primary ColBERT variant '{PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT}' is unavailable. "
   417:                     f"Available={sorted(colbert_variants.keys())}"
   418:                 )
   419:             if np.asarray(colbert_vectors).shape[-1] != 128:
   420:                 raise ValueError("Configured ColBERT vectors are not 128-d.")
   421: 
   422:         chunk["embedding"] = chunk_vector
   423:         chunk["colbert"] = colbert_vectors
   424:         chunk["colbert_variants"] = colbert_variants
   425:         embeddings.append(chunk)
   426: 
   427:     return embeddings
   428:

   321:             continue
   322:         token_count = _token_count(text, tokenizer)
   323:         if token_count <= max_length:
   324:             expanded.append(ch)
   325:             continue
   326:         parts = _split_oversize_text(text, tokenizer, max_length, overlap_tokens)
   327:         part_count = len(parts)
   328:         for idx, part in enumerate(parts, start=1):
   329:             new_chunk = dict(ch)
   330:             new_chunk["chunk"] = part
   331:             new_chunk["text"] = part
   332:             new_chunk["part_index"] = idx
   333:             new_chunk["part_count"] = part_count
   334:             expanded.append(new_chunk)
   335:     return expanded
   336: def embed_chunks(chunks, tokenizer, model, max_length=350):
   337:     print("Starting embedding process...")
   338:     print(f"[embed-config] {_projection_summary()}")
   339: # Our list of embeddings, and runs ColBERT in evaluation mode. Model normally runs in train()   #
   340: # mode, which will drop embeddings and normalize to prevent overfitting.                        #
   341:     embeddings = []
   342:     model.eval()
   343: 
   344:     model_max = getattr(tokenizer, "model_max_length", None)
   345:     effective_max = max_length
   346:     if model_max and isinstance(model_max, int):
   347:         effective_max = min(max_length, model_max)
   348: 
   349:     chunks = _expand_chunks_for_token_limit(
   350:         chunks,
   351:         tokenizer,
   352:         effective_max,
   353:         overlap_tokens=REBALANCE_OVERLAP_TOKENS,
   354:     )
   355: 
   356: # Loop through the chunks based on label, clean text up further, and then tokenize.             #
   357:     progress_every = int(os.environ.get("EMBED_PROGRESS_EVERY", "50"))
   358:     for idx, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
   359:         if progress_every > 0 and (idx == 0 or (idx + 1) % progress_every == 0):
   360:             print(f"Embedding chunk {idx + 1} of {len(chunks)})")
   361:         text = normalize_text_for_embedding(chunk.get("text") or chunk.get("chunk"))
   362: 
   363:         if not text:
   364:             print(f"Empty chunk at index {idx}")
   365:             continue
   366: 
   367: # Call the tokenizer function.                                                                  #
   368:         tokens = tokenizer(
   369:             text,                        # chunk text
   370:             return_tensors="pt",        # change format from lists to pt tensors so ColBERT can read
   371:             truncation=True,            # truncate chunks that go over the token limits
   372:             padding="max_length",       # add zero tokens to make sure tensors are equal size. Helps the model embed faster
   373:             max_length=effective_max     # the token limit
   374:         )
   375:         tokens = {k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in tokens.items()}
   376: 
   377: # Warning message if a chunk was truncated, this means I need to make chunks shorter.           #
   378:         if tokens["input_ids"].shape[1] == effective_max:
   379:             token_count = _token_count(text, tokenizer)
   380:             if token_count > effective_max:
   381:                 print(
   382:                     f"WARNING chunk {chunk.get('section')} truncated from "
   383:                     f"{token_count} tokens to {effective_max}."
   384:                 )
   385: 
   386: # Embed the tokenized text by running it through ColBERT. Tell Pytorch not to track gradients.  #
   387: # stops the collection of tracking data to save a lot of computing time.                        #
   388: # At this point, we now have a set of embeddings for a chunk.                                   #
   389:         with torch.no_grad():
   390:             outputs = model(**tokens)
   391:             token_embeddings = outputs.last_hidden_state.squeeze(0)
   392:             if token_embeddings.shape[-1] != 768:
   393:                 raise ValueError(
   394:                     f"Expected 768-d token embeddings, got {token_embeddings.shape[-1]}"
   395:                 )
   396:             attn_mask = tokens["attention_mask"].squeeze(0).bool()
   397:             masked_embeddings = token_embeddings[attn_mask]
   398:             if masked_embeddings.numel() == 0:
   399:                 raise ValueError("No non-padding tokens available after attention_mask filtering.")
   400:             projected_tokens = _project_tokens_128(masked_embeddings)
   401:             if projected_tokens.shape[-1] != 128:
   402:                 raise ValueError(
   403:                     f"Projected token embeddings must be 128-d. Got {projected_tokens.shape[-1]}."
   404:                 )
   405:             chunk_vector = (
   406:                 projected_tokens.mean(dim=0)
   407:                 .detach()
   408:                 .cpu()
   409:                 .numpy()
   410:                 .tolist()
   411:             )
   412:             colbert_variants = _build_token_variants(projected_tokens)
   413:             colbert_vectors = colbert_variants.get(PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT)
   414:             if colbert_vectors is None:
   415:                 raise ValueError(
   416:                     f"Configured primary ColBERT variant '{PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT}' is unavailable. "
   417:                     f"Available={sorted(colbert_variants.keys())}"
   418:                 )
   419:             if np.asarray(colbert_vectors).shape[-1] != 128:
   420:                 raise ValueError("Configured ColBERT vectors are not 128-d.")
   421: 
   422:         chunk["embedding"] = chunk_vector
   423:         chunk["colbert"] = colbert_vectors
   424:         chunk["colbert_variants"] = colbert_variants
   425:         embeddings.append(chunk)
   426: 
   427:     return embeddings
   428: 
   429: def embed_query(query, tokenizer, model, max_length=256):
   430:     model.eval()
   431:     query_text = normalize_text_for_embedding(query)
   432:  
   433:     tokens = tokenizer(
   434:         query_text,
   435:         return_tensors="pt", 
   436:         truncation=True, 
   437:         padding="max_length", 
   438:         max_length=max_length
   439:         )
   440:     tokens = {k: v.to(DEVICE) for k, v in tokens.items()}
   441:     
   442:     with torch.no_grad():
   443:         outputs = model(**tokens)
   444: 
   445:     token_embeddings = outputs.last_hidden_state.squeeze(0)
   446:     if token_embeddings.shape[-1] != 768:
   447:         raise ValueError(f"Expected query token embeddings [T,768], got {tuple(token_embeddings.shape)}.")

   314: def _colbert_vectors_for_weaviate(emb: dict) -> list[list[float]]:
   315:     """
   316:     Build the multi-vector payload for Weaviate from token-level 128-D ColBERT vectors.
   317:     No fallback is allowed.
   318:     """
   319:     colbert_vectors = emb.get("colbert")
   320:     if colbert_vectors is None:
   321:         variants = emb.get("colbert_variants") or {}
   322:         for key in ("128_f16", "128_f32"):
   323:             if key in variants:
   324:                 colbert_vectors = variants[key]
   325:                 break
   326: 
   327:     if colbert_vectors is None:
   328:         claim_identity = _claim_identity(emb)
   329:         raise ValueError(
   330:             f"Missing token-level ColBERT vectors for claim_id={claim_identity} "
   331:             f"doc_id={emb.get('doc_id', '')}. 768 fallback is forbidden."
   332:         )
   333:     arr = np.asarray(colbert_vectors)
   334:     if arr.ndim == 1:
   335:         arr = arr.reshape(1, -1)
   336:     if arr.ndim != 2 or arr.shape[0] <= 0 or arr.shape[1] != 128:
   337:         claim_identity = _claim_identity(emb)
   338:         raise ValueError(
   339:             f"Invalid token-level ColBERT vectors for claim_id={claim_identity} "
   340:             f"doc_id={emb.get('doc_id', '')}: shape={getattr(arr, 'shape', None)}. "
   341:             "Expected [T,128]."
   342:         )
   343:     return arr.astype(np.float32, copy=False).tolist()
   344: 
   345: 
   346: def _deserialize_colbert(payload: bytes) -> np.ndarray:
   347:     buffer = io.BytesIO(payload)
   348:     obj = np.load(buffer, allow_pickle=False)
   349:     if isinstance(obj, np.lib.npyio.NpzFile):
   350:         data = obj["data"]
   351:         scale = obj["scale"]
   352:         obj.close()
   353:         return data.astype(np.float32) * scale
   354:     return obj
   355: 
   356: 
   357: def _write_lmdb_bytes(lmdb_env: lmdb.Environment, rows: list[tuple[str, bytes]]):
   358:     if not rows:
   359:         return
   360:     while True:
   361:         try:
   362:             with lmdb_env.begin(write=True) as txn:
   363:                 for obj_id, payload in rows:

   319:     colbert_vectors = emb.get("colbert")
   320:     if colbert_vectors is None:
   321:         variants = emb.get("colbert_variants") or {}
   322:         for key in ("128_f16", "128_f32"):
   323:             if key in variants:
   324:                 colbert_vectors = variants[key]
   325:                 break
   326: 
   327:     if colbert_vectors is None:
   328:         claim_identity = _claim_identity(emb)
   329:         raise ValueError(
   330:             f"Missing token-level ColBERT vectors for claim_id={claim_identity} "
   331:             f"doc_id={emb.get('doc_id', '')}. 768 fallback is forbidden."
   332:         )
   333:     arr = np.asarray(colbert_vectors)
   334:     if arr.ndim == 1:
   335:         arr = arr.reshape(1, -1)
   336:     if arr.ndim != 2 or arr.shape[0] <= 0 or arr.shape[1] != 128:
   337:         claim_identity = _claim_identity(emb)
   338:         raise ValueError(
   339:             f"Invalid token-level ColBERT vectors for claim_id={claim_identity} "
   340:             f"doc_id={emb.get('doc_id', '')}: shape={getattr(arr, 'shape', None)}. "
   341:             "Expected [T,128]."
   342:         )
   343:     return arr.astype(np.float32, copy=False).tolist()
   344: 
   345: 
   346: def _deserialize_colbert(payload: bytes) -> np.ndarray:
   347:     buffer = io.BytesIO(payload)
   348:     obj = np.load(buffer, allow_pickle=False)
   349:     if isinstance(obj, np.lib.npyio.NpzFile):
   350:         data = obj["data"]
   351:         scale = obj["scale"]
   352:         obj.close()
   353:         return data.astype(np.float32) * scale
   354:     return obj
   355:

   363:                 for obj_id, payload in rows:

   579:         current_props = getattr(cfg, "properties", None) or []
   580:         existing = {str(getattr(p, "name", "")) for p in current_props if getattr(p, "name", None)}

   582:             prop_name = str(getattr(prop, "name", "") or "")
   583:             if not prop_name or prop_name in existing:
   584:                 continue

   586:                 collection.config.add_property(prop)
   587:                 print(f"[config] Added missing property {collection_name}.{prop_name}")
   588:             except Exception as e:
   589:                 print(f"[warn] Failed to add property {collection_name}.{prop_name}: {e}")

   591:         print(f"[warn] Could not verify properties for collection {collection_name}: {e}")

   597:     patent_props = [
   598:         Property(name="doc_id", data_type=DataType.TEXT),
   599:         Property(name="filing_date", data_type=DataType.TEXT),
   600:         Property(name="classification", data_type=DataType.TEXT),
   601:         Property(name="authors", data_type=DataType.TEXT_ARRAY),
   602:         Property(name="title", data_type=DataType.TEXT),
   603:         Property(name="kind", data_type=DataType.TEXT),
   604:     ]
   605:     if "Patent" not in existing:
   606:         client.collections.create(
   607:             name="Patent",
   608:             vectorizer_config=Configure.Vectorizer.none(),
   609:             properties=patent_props,
   610:         )
   611:         print("Created collection: Patent")
   612:     else:
   613:         print("Collection Patent already exists")
   614:         _ensure_collection_properties(client, "Patent", patent_props)

   621:         Property(name="text", data_type=DataType.TEXT),

   624:         try:

   575: def _ensure_collection_properties(client, collection_name: str, props: list[Property]):
   576:     try:
   577:         collection = client.collections.get(collection_name)
   578:         cfg = collection.config.get()
   579:         current_props = getattr(cfg, "properties", None) or []
   580:         existing = {str(getattr(p, "name", "")) for p in current_props if getattr(p, "name", None)}
   581:         for prop in props:
   582:             prop_name = str(getattr(prop, "name", "") or "")
   583:             if not prop_name or prop_name in existing:
   584:                 continue
   585:             try:
   586:                 collection.config.add_property(prop)
   587:                 print(f"[config] Added missing property {collection_name}.{prop_name}")
   588:             except Exception as e:
   589:                 print(f"[warn] Failed to add property {collection_name}.{prop_name}: {e}")
   590:     except Exception as e:
   591:         print(f"[warn] Could not verify properties for collection {collection_name}: {e}")

   758:                 claim_text = str(emb.get("text") or emb.get("chunk") or "")
   759:                 full_vectors = _select_full_colbert_vectors_for_lmdb(emb)
   760:                 if full_vectors is not None and full_vectors.size:
   761:                     claim_payload_rows.append(
   762:                         (
   763:                             str(claim_key),
   764:                             {
   765:                                 "text": claim_text,
   766:                                 "doc_id": doc_id,
   767:                                 "claim_type": str(emb.get("claim_type") or ""),
   768:                                 "claim_number": emb.get("claim_number"),
   769:                                 "colbert": full_vectors,
   770:                             },
   771:                         )
   772:                     )
   773: 
   774:                 if WRITE_LMDB:
   775:                     colbert_variants = emb.get("colbert_variants") or {}
   776:                     if colbert_variants:
   777:                         for name, vecs in colbert_variants.items():
   778:                             name = str(name).lower()
   779:                             if "768" in name:
   780:                                 raise ValueError(f"Forbidden 768 variant encountered during ingest: {name}")
   781:                             if name not in LMDB_WRITE_VARIANTS:
   782:                                 continue
   783:                             size = getattr(vecs, "size", None)
   784:                             if size is None:
   785:                                 try:
   786:                                     size = len(vecs)
   787:                                 except Exception:
   788:                                     size = 0
