   790:                                 key = str(claim_key)
   791:                                 lmdb_path = resolve_lmdb_path(
   792:                                     name,
   793:                                     doc_id=doc_id,
   794:                                     dataset_shard=dataset_shard,
   795:                                 )
   796:                                 lmdb_rows_by_path.setdefault(lmdb_path, []).append((key, vecs))
   797:                     else:
   798:                         colbert_vectors = emb.get("colbert")
   799:                         if colbert_vectors is not None:
   800:                             size = getattr(colbert_vectors, "size", None)
   801:                             if size is None:
   802:                                 try:
   803:                                     size = len(colbert_vectors)
   804:                                 except Exception:
   805:                                     size = 0
   806:                             if size:
   807:                                 key = str(claim_key)
   808:                                 lmdb_path = resolve_lmdb_path(
   809:                                     PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT,
   810:                                     doc_id=doc_id,
   811:                                     dataset_shard=dataset_shard,
   812:                                 )
   813:                                 lmdb_rows_by_path.setdefault(lmdb_path, []).append((key, colbert_vectors))

   815:             if patent_metadata_rows:
   816:                 env = lmdb_envs_by_path.get(LMDB_PATH_PATENT_METADATA)
   817:                 if env is None:
   818:                     env = _open_lmdb_env(LMDB_PATH_PATENT_METADATA)
   819:                     lmdb_envs_by_path[LMDB_PATH_PATENT_METADATA] = env
   820:                 _write_lmdb_json_records(env, patent_metadata_rows)

   823:                 env = lmdb_envs_by_path.get(LMDB_PATH_CLAIM_PAYLOAD)
   824:                 if env is None:
   825:                     env = _open_lmdb_env(LMDB_PATH_CLAIM_PAYLOAD)

   836:                         _write_lmdb_vectors(env, rows)

   838:         print(f"Uploaded {len(embeddings)} claims to database.")
   839:     finally:
   840:         for env in lmdb_envs_by_path.values():
   841:             env.close()

   655: def store_embeddings(embeddings):

   661:         patent_collection = client.collections.get("Patent")
   662:

   665:             mv = None

   672:                         break

   675:             if not mv or not vector_index_cfg or not multivector_cfg:
   676:                 raise RuntimeError(
   677:                     "Collection 'Claim' is not configured for multi-vector 'colbert'. "
   678:                     "Set DROP_FIRST=True and recreate the collection."
   679:                 )
   680:         except Exception:
   681:             raise RuntimeError(
   682:                 "Unable to verify multi-vector config for 'colbert' in Claim. "
   683:                 "Upgrade weaviate-client and recreate the collection."

   698:                 "filing_date": emb.get("filing_date", ""),

   700:                 "authors": authors if isinstance(authors, list) else [],
   701:                 "title": emb.get("title", ""),
   702:                 "kind": emb.get("kind", ""),
   703:             }
   704: 
   705:         if patent_by_doc:

   707:                 DataObject(
   708:                     properties=props,
   709:                     uuid=_stable_uuid("patent", str(props.get("doc_id", ""))),
   710:                 )
   711:                 for props in patent_by_doc.values()
   712:             ]
   713:             result = patent_collection.data.insert_many(patent_objects)
   714:             if result.has_errors:
   715:                 print(f"[warn] patent batch insert had {len(result.errors)} error(s)")

   726:                     "claim_id": emb.get("claim_id", "") or claim_identity,

   728:                     "doc_id": emb.get("doc_id", ""),
   729:                     "text": emb.get("text") or emb.get("chunk", ""),

   828:

   830:                 for lmdb_path, rows in lmdb_rows_by_path.items():
   831:                     if rows:
   832:                         env = lmdb_envs_by_path.get(lmdb_path)

   834:                             env = _open_lmdb_env(lmdb_path)
   835:                             lmdb_envs_by_path[lmdb_path] = env
   836:                         _write_lmdb_vectors(env, rows)

   839:     finally:
   840:         for env in lmdb_envs_by_path.values():
   841:             env.close()

   655: def store_embeddings(embeddings):
   656:     client = get_client()
   657:     lmdb_envs_by_path: dict[Path, lmdb.Environment] = {}
   658:     try:
   659:         ensure_collection(client)
   660:         claim_collection = client.collections.get("Claim")
   661:         patent_collection = client.collections.get("Patent")
   662: 
   663:         try:
   664:             cfg = claim_collection.config.get().vector_config
   665:             mv = None
   666:             if isinstance(cfg, dict):
   667:                 mv = cfg.get("colbert")
   668:             elif isinstance(cfg, list):
   669:                 for item in cfg:
   670:                     if getattr(item, "name", None) == "colbert":
   671:                         mv = item
   672:                         break
   673:             vector_index_cfg = getattr(mv, "vector_index_config", None) or getattr(mv, "vectorIndexConfig", None)
   674:             multivector_cfg = getattr(vector_index_cfg, "multi_vector", None) or getattr(vector_index_cfg, "multivector", None)
   675:             if not mv or not vector_index_cfg or not multivector_cfg:
   676:                 raise RuntimeError(
   677:                     "Collection 'Claim' is not configured for multi-vector 'colbert'. "
   678:                     "Set DROP_FIRST=True and recreate the collection."
   679:                 )
   680:         except Exception:
   681:             raise RuntimeError(
   682:                 "Unable to verify multi-vector config for 'colbert' in Claim. "
   683:                 "Upgrade weaviate-client and recreate the collection."
   684:             )
   685: 
   686:         print(f"Uploading {len(embeddings)} claim embeddings...")
   687:         written_doc_ids: set[str] = set()
   688:         patent_by_doc: dict[str, dict] = {}
   689:         for emb in embeddings:
   690:             doc_id = str(emb.get("doc_id") or "").strip()
   691:             if not doc_id or doc_id in patent_by_doc:
   692:                 continue
   693:             authors = emb.get("authors", [])
   694:             if isinstance(authors, str):
   695:                 authors = [a.strip() for a in authors.split(";") if a.strip()]
   696:             patent_by_doc[doc_id] = {
   697:                 "doc_id": doc_id,
   698:                 "filing_date": emb.get("filing_date", ""),
   699:                 "classification": emb.get("classification", ""),
   700:                 "authors": authors if isinstance(authors, list) else [],
   701:                 "title": emb.get("title", ""),
   702:                 "kind": emb.get("kind", ""),
   703:             }
   704: 
   705:         if patent_by_doc:
   706:             patent_objects = [
   707:                 DataObject(
   708:                     properties=props,
   709:                     uuid=_stable_uuid("patent", str(props.get("doc_id", ""))),
   710:                 )
   711:                 for props in patent_by_doc.values()
   712:             ]
   713:             result = patent_collection.data.insert_many(patent_objects)
   714:             if result.has_errors:
   715:                 print(f"[warn] patent batch insert had {len(result.errors)} error(s)")
   716: 
   717:         for start in range(0, len(embeddings), WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE):
   718:             batch = embeddings[start:start + WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE]
   719:             objects = []
   720:             for emb in batch:
   721:                 claim_identity = _claim_identity(emb)
   722:                 claim_number = emb.get("claim_number")
   723:                 if isinstance(claim_number, str) and claim_number.isdigit():
   724:                     claim_number = int(claim_number)
   725:                 props = {
   726:                     "claim_id": emb.get("claim_id", "") or claim_identity,
   727:                     "claim_type": emb.get("claim_type", ""),
   728:                     "doc_id": emb.get("doc_id", ""),
   729:                     "text": emb.get("text") or emb.get("chunk", ""),
   730:                 }
   731:                 if isinstance(claim_number, int):
   732:                     props["claim_number"] = claim_number
   733:                 vecs = {"colbert": _colbert_vectors_for_weaviate(emb)}
   734:                 objects.append(
   735:                     DataObject(
   736:                         properties=props,
   737:                         vector=vecs,
   738:                         uuid=_stable_uuid("claim", claim_identity),
   739:                     )
   740:                 )
   741: 
   742:             result = claim_collection.data.insert_many(objects)
   743:             if result.has_errors:
   744:                 print(f"[warn] batch insert had {len(result.errors)} error(s)")
   745: 
   746:             lmdb_rows_by_path: dict[Path, list[tuple[str, list]]] = {}
   747:             patent_metadata_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   748:             claim_payload_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   749:             for emb in batch:
   750:                 claim_key = _claim_identity(emb)
   751:                 doc_id = str(emb.get("doc_id") or "").strip()
   752:                 dataset_shard = emb.get("dataset_shard")
   753: 
   754:                 if doc_id and doc_id not in written_doc_ids:
   755:                     written_doc_ids.add(doc_id)
   756:                     patent_metadata_rows.append((doc_id, _build_patent_metadata_record(emb)))
   757: 
   758:                 claim_text = str(emb.get("text") or emb.get("chunk") or "")
   759:                 full_vectors = _select_full_colbert_vectors_for_lmdb(emb)

   788:                                     size = 0

   665:             mv = None
   666:             if isinstance(cfg, dict):

   669:                 for item in cfg:

   671:                         mv = item
   672:                         break

   675:             if not mv or not vector_index_cfg or not multivector_cfg:
   676:                 raise RuntimeError(

   678:                     "Set DROP_FIRST=True and recreate the collection."
   679:                 )
   680:         except Exception:
   681:             raise RuntimeError(

   698:                 "filing_date": emb.get("filing_date", ""),
   699:                 "classification": emb.get("classification", ""),
   700:                 "authors": authors if isinstance(authors, list) else [],
   701:                 "title": emb.get("title", ""),
   702:                 "kind": emb.get("kind", ""),
   703:             }
   704: 
   705:         if patent_by_doc:
   706:             patent_objects = [
   707:                 DataObject(
   708:                     properties=props,
   709:                     uuid=_stable_uuid("patent", str(props.get("doc_id", ""))),
   710:                 )
   711:                 for props in patent_by_doc.values()
   712:             ]
   713:             result = patent_collection.data.insert_many(patent_objects)
   714:             if result.has_errors:
   715:                 print(f"[warn] patent batch insert had {len(result.errors)} error(s)")

   717:         for start in range(0, len(embeddings), WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE):
   718:             batch = embeddings[start:start + WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE]

   721:                 claim_identity = _claim_identity(emb)

   723:                 if isinstance(claim_number, str) and claim_number.isdigit():
   724:                     claim_number = int(claim_number)
   725:                 props = {
   726:                     "claim_id": emb.get("claim_id", "") or claim_identity,
   727:                     "claim_type": emb.get("claim_type", ""),
   728:                     "doc_id": emb.get("doc_id", ""),
   729:                     "text": emb.get("text") or emb.get("chunk", ""),
   730:                 }
   731:                 if isinstance(claim_number, int):
   732:                     props["claim_number"] = claim_number
   733:                 vecs = {"colbert": _colbert_vectors_for_weaviate(emb)}
   734:                 objects.append(
   735:                     DataObject(
   736:                         properties=props,
   737:                         vector=vecs,
   738:                         uuid=_stable_uuid("claim", claim_identity),
   739:                     )
   740:                 )
   741: 
   742:             result = claim_collection.data.insert_many(objects)
   743:             if result.has_errors:
   744:                 print(f"[warn] batch insert had {len(result.errors)} error(s)")
   745: 
   746:             lmdb_rows_by_path: dict[Path, list[tuple[str, list]]] = {}
   747:             patent_metadata_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   748:             claim_payload_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   749:             for emb in batch:
   750:                 claim_key = _claim_identity(emb)
   751:                 doc_id = str(emb.get("doc_id") or "").strip()
   752:                 dataset_shard = emb.get("dataset_shard")
   753: 
   754:                 if doc_id and doc_id not in written_doc_ids:
   755:                     written_doc_ids.add(doc_id)

   788:                                     size = 0

   661:         patent_collection = client.collections.get("Patent")
   662: 
   663:         try:
   664:             cfg = claim_collection.config.get().vector_config
   665:             mv = None
   666:             if isinstance(cfg, dict):
   667:                 mv = cfg.get("colbert")
   668:             elif isinstance(cfg, list):
   669:                 for item in cfg:
   670:                     if getattr(item, "name", None) == "colbert":
   671:                         mv = item
   672:                         break
   673:             vector_index_cfg = getattr(mv, "vector_index_config", None) or getattr(mv, "vectorIndexConfig", None)
   674:             multivector_cfg = getattr(vector_index_cfg, "multi_vector", None) or getattr(vector_index_cfg, "multivector", None)
   675:             if not mv or not vector_index_cfg or not multivector_cfg:
   676:                 raise RuntimeError(
   677:                     "Collection 'Claim' is not configured for multi-vector 'colbert'. "
   678:                     "Set DROP_FIRST=True and recreate the collection."
   679:                 )
   680:         except Exception:
   681:             raise RuntimeError(
   682:                 "Unable to verify multi-vector config for 'colbert' in Claim. "
   683:                 "Upgrade weaviate-client and recreate the collection."
   684:             )
   685: 
   686:         print(f"Uploading {len(embeddings)} claim embeddings...")
   687:         written_doc_ids: set[str] = set()
   688:         patent_by_doc: dict[str, dict] = {}
   689:         for emb in embeddings:
   690:             doc_id = str(emb.get("doc_id") or "").strip()
   691:             if not doc_id or doc_id in patent_by_doc:
   692:                 continue
   693:             authors = emb.get("authors", [])
   694:             if isinstance(authors, str):
   695:                 authors = [a.strip() for a in authors.split(";") if a.strip()]
   696:             patent_by_doc[doc_id] = {
   697:                 "doc_id": doc_id,
   698:                 "filing_date": emb.get("filing_date", ""),
   699:                 "classification": emb.get("classification", ""),
   700:                 "authors": authors if isinstance(authors, list) else [],
   701:                 "title": emb.get("title", ""),
   702:                 "kind": emb.get("kind", ""),
   703:             }
   704: 
   705:         if patent_by_doc:
   706:             patent_objects = [
   707:                 DataObject(
   708:                     properties=props,
   709:                     uuid=_stable_uuid("patent", str(props.get("doc_id", ""))),
   710:                 )
   711:                 for props in patent_by_doc.values()
   712:             ]
   713:             result = patent_collection.data.insert_many(patent_objects)
   714:             if result.has_errors:
   715:                 print(f"[warn] patent batch insert had {len(result.errors)} error(s)")
   716: 
   717:         for start in range(0, len(embeddings), WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE):
   718:             batch = embeddings[start:start + WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE]
   719:             objects = []
   720:             for emb in batch:
   721:                 claim_identity = _claim_identity(emb)
   722:                 claim_number = emb.get("claim_number")
   723:                 if isinstance(claim_number, str) and claim_number.isdigit():
   724:                     claim_number = int(claim_number)
   725:                 props = {
   726:                     "claim_id": emb.get("claim_id", "") or claim_identity,
   727:                     "claim_type": emb.get("claim_type", ""),
   728:                     "doc_id": emb.get("doc_id", ""),
   729:                     "text": emb.get("text") or emb.get("chunk", ""),
   730:                 }
   731:                 if isinstance(claim_number, int):
   732:                     props["claim_number"] = claim_number
   733:                 vecs = {"colbert": _colbert_vectors_for_weaviate(emb)}
   734:                 objects.append(
   735:                     DataObject(
   736:                         properties=props,
   737:                         vector=vecs,
   738:                         uuid=_stable_uuid("claim", claim_identity),
   739:                     )
   740:                 )
   741: 
   742:             result = claim_collection.data.insert_many(objects)
   743:             if result.has_errors:
   744:                 print(f"[warn] batch insert had {len(result.errors)} error(s)")
   745: 
   746:             lmdb_rows_by_path: dict[Path, list[tuple[str, list]]] = {}
   747:             patent_metadata_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   748:             claim_payload_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   749:             for emb in batch:
   750:                 claim_key = _claim_identity(emb)
   751:                 doc_id = str(emb.get("doc_id") or "").strip()
   752:                 dataset_shard = emb.get("dataset_shard")
   753: 
   754:                 if doc_id and doc_id not in written_doc_ids:
   755:                     written_doc_ids.add(doc_id)
   756:                     patent_metadata_rows.append((doc_id, _build_patent_metadata_record(emb)))
   757: 
   758:                 claim_text = str(emb.get("text") or emb.get("chunk") or "")
   759:                 full_vectors = _select_full_colbert_vectors_for_lmdb(emb)
   760:                 if full_vectors is not None and full_vectors.size:
   761:                     claim_payload_rows.append(
   762:                         (
   763:                             str(claim_key),
   764:                             {
   765:                                 "text": claim_text,
   766:                                 "doc_id": doc_id,
   767:                                 "claim_type": str(emb.get("claim_type") or ""),
   768:                                 "claim_number": emb.get("claim_number"),
   769:                                 "colbert": full_vectors,
   770:                             },
   771:                         )
   772:                     )
   773: 
   774:                 if WRITE_LMDB:
   775:                     colbert_variants = emb.get("colbert_variants") or {}
   776:                     if colbert_variants:
   777:                         for name, vecs in colbert_variants.items():
   778:                             name = str(name).lower()
   779:                             if "768" in name:
   780:                                 raise ValueError(f"Forbidden 768 variant encountered during ingest: {name}")
   781:                             if name not in LMDB_WRITE_VARIANTS:
   782:                                 continue
   783:                             size = getattr(vecs, "size", None)
   784:                             if size is None:
   785:                                 try:
   786:                                     size = len(vecs)
   787:                                 except Exception:
   788:                                     size = 0
   789:                             if size:
   790:                                 key = str(claim_key)
   791:                                 lmdb_path = resolve_lmdb_path(
   792:                                     name,
   793:                                     doc_id=doc_id,
   794:                                     dataset_shard=dataset_shard,
   795:                                 )
   796:                                 lmdb_rows_by_path.setdefault(lmdb_path, []).append((key, vecs))
   797:                     else:
   798:                         colbert_vectors = emb.get("colbert")
   799:                         if colbert_vectors is not None:
   800:                             size = getattr(colbert_vectors, "size", None)
   801:                             if size is None:
   802:                                 try:
   803:                                     size = len(colbert_vectors)
   804:                                 except Exception:
   805:                                     size = 0
   806:                             if size:
   807:                                 key = str(claim_key)
   808:                                 lmdb_path = resolve_lmdb_path(
   809:                                     PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT,
   810:                                     doc_id=doc_id,
   811:                                     dataset_shard=dataset_shard,
   812:                                 )
   813:                                 lmdb_rows_by_path.setdefault(lmdb_path, []).append((key, colbert_vectors))
   814: 
   815:             if patent_metadata_rows:
   816:                 env = lmdb_envs_by_path.get(LMDB_PATH_PATENT_METADATA)
   817:                 if env is None:
   818:                     env = _open_lmdb_env(LMDB_PATH_PATENT_METADATA)
   819:                     lmdb_envs_by_path[LMDB_PATH_PATENT_METADATA] = env
   820:                 _write_lmdb_json_records(env, patent_metadata_rows)
   821: 
   822:             if claim_payload_rows:
   823:                 env = lmdb_envs_by_path.get(LMDB_PATH_CLAIM_PAYLOAD)
   824:                 if env is None:
   825:                     env = _open_lmdb_env(LMDB_PATH_CLAIM_PAYLOAD)
   826:                     lmdb_envs_by_path[LMDB_PATH_CLAIM_PAYLOAD] = env
   827:                 _write_lmdb_claim_payloads(env, claim_payload_rows)
   828: 
   829:             if WRITE_LMDB:
   830:                 for lmdb_path, rows in lmdb_rows_by_path.items():
   831:                     if rows:
   832:                         env = lmdb_envs_by_path.get(lmdb_path)
   833:                         if env is None:
   834:                             env = _open_lmdb_env(lmdb_path)
   835:                             lmdb_envs_by_path[lmdb_path] = env
   836:                         _write_lmdb_vectors(env, rows)
   837: 
   838:         print(f"Uploaded {len(embeddings)} claims to database.")
   839:     finally:
   840:         for env in lmdb_envs_by_path.values():
   841:             env.close()
   842:         client.close()

   696:             patent_by_doc[doc_id] = {
   697:                 "doc_id": doc_id,
   698:                 "filing_date": emb.get("filing_date", ""),
   699:                 "classification": emb.get("classification", ""),
   700:                 "authors": authors if isinstance(authors, list) else [],
   701:                 "title": emb.get("title", ""),
   702:                 "kind": emb.get("kind", ""),
   703:             }
   704: 
   705:         if patent_by_doc:
   706:             patent_objects = [
   707:                 DataObject(
   708:                     properties=props,
   709:                     uuid=_stable_uuid("patent", str(props.get("doc_id", ""))),
   710:                 )
   711:                 for props in patent_by_doc.values()
   712:             ]
   713:             result = patent_collection.data.insert_many(patent_objects)
   714:             if result.has_errors:
   715:                 print(f"[warn] patent batch insert had {len(result.errors)} error(s)")
   716: 
   717:         for start in range(0, len(embeddings), WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE):
   718:             batch = embeddings[start:start + WEAVIATE_BATCH_SIZE]
   719:             objects = []
   720:             for emb in batch:
   721:                 claim_identity = _claim_identity(emb)
   722:                 claim_number = emb.get("claim_number")
   723:                 if isinstance(claim_number, str) and claim_number.isdigit():
   724:                     claim_number = int(claim_number)
   725:                 props = {
   726:                     "claim_id": emb.get("claim_id", "") or claim_identity,
   727:                     "claim_type": emb.get("claim_type", ""),
   728:                     "doc_id": emb.get("doc_id", ""),
   729:                     "text": emb.get("text") or emb.get("chunk", ""),
   730:                 }
   731:                 if isinstance(claim_number, int):
   732:                     props["claim_number"] = claim_number
   733:                 vecs = {"colbert": _colbert_vectors_for_weaviate(emb)}
   734:                 objects.append(
   735:                     DataObject(
   736:                         properties=props,
   737:                         vector=vecs,
   738:                         uuid=_stable_uuid("claim", claim_identity),
   739:                     )
   740:                 )
   741: 
   742:             result = claim_collection.data.insert_many(objects)
   743:             if result.has_errors:
   744:                 print(f"[warn] batch insert had {len(result.errors)} error(s)")
   745: 
   746:             lmdb_rows_by_path: dict[Path, list[tuple[str, list]]] = {}
   747:             patent_metadata_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   748:             claim_payload_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   749:             for emb in batch:
   750:                 claim_key = _claim_identity(emb)
   751:                 doc_id = str(emb.get("doc_id") or "").strip()
   752:                 dataset_shard = emb.get("dataset_shard")
   753: 
   754:                 if doc_id and doc_id not in written_doc_ids:
   755:                     written_doc_ids.add(doc_id)
   756:                     patent_metadata_rows.append((doc_id, _build_patent_metadata_record(emb)))
   757: 
   758:                 claim_text = str(emb.get("text") or emb.get("chunk") or "")
   759:                 full_vectors = _select_full_colbert_vectors_for_lmdb(emb)
   760:                 if full_vectors is not None and full_vectors.size:
   761:                     claim_payload_rows.append(
   762:                         (
   763:                             str(claim_key),
   764:                             {
   765:                                 "text": claim_text,
   766:                                 "doc_id": doc_id,
   767:                                 "claim_type": str(emb.get("claim_type") or ""),
   768:                                 "claim_number": emb.get("claim_number"),
   769:                                 "colbert": full_vectors,
   770:                             },
   771:                         )
   772:                     )
   773: 
   774:                 if WRITE_LMDB:
   775:                     colbert_variants = emb.get("colbert_variants") or {}
   776:                     if colbert_variants:
   777:                         for name, vecs in colbert_variants.items():
   778:                             name = str(name).lower()
   779:                             if "768" in name:
   780:                                 raise ValueError(f"Forbidden 768 variant encountered during ingest: {name}")
   781:                             if name not in LMDB_WRITE_VARIANTS:
   782:                                 continue
   783:                             size = getattr(vecs, "size", None)
   784:                             if size is None:
   785:                                 try:
   786:                                     size = len(vecs)
   787:                                 except Exception:
   788:                                     size = 0
   789:                             if size:
   790:                                 key = str(claim_key)
   791:                                 lmdb_path = resolve_lmdb_path(
   792:                                     name,
   793:                                     doc_id=doc_id,
   794:                                     dataset_shard=dataset_shard,
   795:                                 )
   796:                                 lmdb_rows_by_path.setdefault(lmdb_path, []).append((key, vecs))
   797:                     else:
   798:                         colbert_vectors = emb.get("colbert")
   799:                         if colbert_vectors is not None:
   800:                             size = getattr(colbert_vectors, "size", None)
   801:                             if size is None:
   802:                                 try:
   803:                                     size = len(colbert_vectors)
   804:                                 except Exception:
   805:                                     size = 0
   806:                             if size:
   807:                                 key = str(claim_key)
   808:                                 lmdb_path = resolve_lmdb_path(
   809:                                     PRIMARY_COLBERT_VARIANT,
   810:                                     doc_id=doc_id,
   811:                                     dataset_shard=dataset_shard,
   812:                                 )
   813:                                 lmdb_rows_by_path.setdefault(lmdb_path, []).append((key, colbert_vectors))
   814: 
   815:             if patent_metadata_rows:
   816:                 env = lmdb_envs_by_path.get(LMDB_PATH_PATENT_METADATA)
   817:                 if env is None:
   818:                     env = _open_lmdb_env(LMDB_PATH_PATENT_METADATA)
   819:                     lmdb_envs_by_path[LMDB_PATH_PATENT_METADATA] = env
   820:                 _write_lmdb_json_records(env, patent_metadata_rows)
   821: 
   822:             if claim_payload_rows:
   823:                 env = lmdb_envs_by_path.get(LMDB_PATH_CLAIM_PAYLOAD)
   824:                 if env is None:
   825:                     env = _open_lmdb_env(LMDB_PATH_CLAIM_PAYLOAD)
   826:                     lmdb_envs_by_path[LMDB_PATH_CLAIM_PAYLOAD] = env
   827:                 _write_lmdb_claim_payloads(env, claim_payload_rows)
   828: 
   829:             if WRITE_LMDB:
   830:                 for lmdb_path, rows in lmdb_rows_by_path.items():
   831:                     if rows:
   832:                         env = lmdb_envs_by_path.get(lmdb_path)
   833:                         if env is None:
   834:                             env = _open_lmdb_env(lmdb_path)
   835:                             lmdb_envs_by_path[lmdb_path] = env
   836:                         _write_lmdb_vectors(env, rows)
   837: 
   838:         print(f"Uploaded {len(embeddings)} claims to database.")
   839:     finally:
   840:         for env in lmdb_envs_by_path.values():
   841:             env.close()
   842:         client.close()

   731:                 if isinstance(claim_number, int):
   732:                     props["claim_number"] = claim_number
   733:                 vecs = {"colbert": _colbert_vectors_for_weaviate(emb)}
   734:                 objects.append(
   735:                     DataObject(
   736:                         properties=props,
   737:                         vector=vecs,
   738:                         uuid=_stable_uuid("claim", claim_identity),
   739:                     )
   740:                 )
   741: 
   742:             result = claim_collection.data.insert_many(objects)
   743:             if result.has_errors:
   744:                 print(f"[warn] batch insert had {len(result.errors)} error(s)")

   746:             lmdb_rows_by_path: dict[Path, list[tuple[str, list]]] = {}
   747:             patent_metadata_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   748:             claim_payload_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   749:             for emb in batch:
   750:                 claim_key = _claim_identity(emb)
   751:                 doc_id = str(emb.get("doc_id") or "").strip()
   752:                 dataset_shard = emb.get("dataset_shard")
   753: 
   754:                 if doc_id and doc_id not in written_doc_ids:
   755:                     written_doc_ids.add(doc_id)
   756:                     patent_metadata_rows.append((doc_id, _build_patent_metadata_record(emb)))

   758:                 claim_text = str(emb.get("text") or emb.get("chunk") or "")

   760:                 if full_vectors is not None and full_vectors.size:
   761:                     claim_payload_rows.append(

   766:                                 "doc_id": doc_id,

   768:                                 "claim_number": emb.get("claim_number"),
   769:                                 "colbert": full_vectors,
   770:                             },
   771:                         )
   772:                     )
   773: 
   774:                 if WRITE_LMDB:
   775:                     colbert_variants = emb.get("colbert_variants") or {}
   776:                     if colbert_variants:
   777:                         for name, vecs in colbert_variants.items():
   778:                             name = str(name).lower()
   779:                             if "768" in name:
   780:                                 raise ValueError(f"Forbidden 768 variant encountered during ingest: {name}")
   781:                             if name not in LMDB_WRITE_VARIANTS:

   804:                                 except Exception:
   805:                                     size = 0

   812:                                 )

   817:                 if env is None:

   838:         print(f"Uploaded {len(embeddings)} claims to database.")

    47: def _deserialize_colbert(payload: bytes) -> np.ndarray:
    48:     buffer = io.BytesIO(payload)
    49:     obj = np.load(buffer, allow_pickle=False)
    50:     if isinstance(obj, np.lib.npyio.NpzFile):
    51:         data = obj["data"]
    52:         scale = obj["scale"]
    53:         obj.close()
    54:         return data.astype(np.float32) * scale
    55:     return obj

   201:         def _write_row(row: dict):

   205:         def _flush_pending(force: bool = False):
   206:             nonlocal pending
   207:             if not pending:
   208:                 return
   209:             if force:
   210:                 done = pending
   211:                 pending = []
   212:             else:
   213:                 done_set, _ = wait(
   214:                     [fut for fut, _ in pending],
   215:                     return_when=FIRST_COMPLETED,
   216:                 )
   217:                 keep: list[tuple[object, dict]] = []
   218:                 done: list[tuple[object, dict]] = []
   219:                 for fut, row in pending:
   220:                     if fut in done_set:
   221:                         done.append((fut, row))
   222:                     else:
   223:                         keep.append((fut, row))
   224:                 pending = keep
   225:

   228:                     fut.result()
   229:                     counts["updated"] += 1
   230:                     row["status"] = "updated"

   235:                     if args.strict:

   238:                 _write_row(row)

   304:                                     counts["updated"] += 1
   305:                             else:
   306:                                 counts["updated"] += 1
   307:             except Exception as e:
   308:                 counts["errors"] += 1
   309:                 status = "error"
   310:                 message = str(e)
   311:                 if args.strict:
   312:                     raise
   313: 
   314:             if args.dry_run or patch_executor is None or status != "updated":
   315:                 _write_row(
   316:                     {
   317:                         "uuid": uuid,
   318:                         "claim_id": claim_id,
   319:                         "doc_id": doc_id,
   320:                         "lmdb_path": lmdb_path,
   321:                         "tokens": token_count,
   322:                         "dim": dim,
   323:                         "status": status,
   324:                         "message": message,
   325:                     }
   326:                 )
   327: 
   328:             if args.progress_every > 0 and (
   329:                 counts["seen"] == 1 or counts["seen"] % args.progress_every == 0
   330:             ):
   331:                 in_flight = len(pending) if patch_executor is not None else 0
   332:                 print(
   333:                     f"[backfill] seen={counts['seen']} updated={counts['updated']} "
   334:                     f"in_flight={in_flight} missing_lmdb={counts['missing_lmdb']} "
   335:                     f"errors={counts['errors']}"
   336:                 )
   337:         _flush_pending(force=True)
   338:     finally:
   339:         try:
   340:             if "patch_executor" in locals() and patch_executor is not None:
   341:                 patch_executor.shutdown(wait=True)
   342:         except Exception:
   343:             pass
   344:         reader.close()
   345:         if csv_f is not None:
   346:             csv_f.close()
   347: 
   348:     mode = "dry-run" if args.dry_run else "apply"
   349:     print(
   350:         f"[backfill] done mode={mode} seen={counts['seen']} updated={counts['updated']} "
   351:         f"missing_claim_id={counts['missing_claim_id']} missing_lmdb={counts['missing_lmdb']} "
   352:         f"invalid_vectors={counts['invalid_vectors']} errors={counts['errors']}"
   353:     )
   354:     if args.report_csv:
   355:         print(f"[backfill] wrote report: {args.report_csv}")
   356: 
   357: 
   358: if __name__ == "__main__":
   359:     main()

   213:     gql = (
   214:         "{ Get { Claim("
   215:         f"{retrieval_args}"
   216:         ") { claim_id doc_id _additional { id distance } } } }"

   221: 
   222: def fetch_colbert_vectors_from_weaviate(object_ids: list[str]) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
   223:     """
   224:     Fetch token-level ColBERT vectors from Weaviate object REST API.
   225:     Endpoint used: GET /v1/objects/Claim/{uuid}?include=vector
   226:     """
   227:     ids = [str(oid).strip() for oid in object_ids if str(oid).strip()]
   228:     if not ids:
   229:         return {}
   230:     sess = requests.Session()
   231:     out: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
   232:     for oid in ids:
   233:         url = f"{WEAVIATE_OBJECTS}/Claim/{oid}"
   234:         resp = sess.get(url, params={"include": "vector"}, timeout=60)
   235:         if resp.status_code != 200:
   236:             raise RuntimeError(f"Weaviate vector fetch failed for {oid}: {resp.status_code} {resp.text[:300]}")
   237:         payload = resp.json()
   238:         vectors = payload.get("vectors")
   239:         if not isinstance(vectors, dict):
   240:             raise ValueError(
   241:                 f"Weaviate object {oid} missing named vectors map; expected dict with key '{WEAVIATE_NAMED_VECTOR}'."
   242:             )
   243:         if WEAVIATE_NAMED_VECTOR not in vectors:
   244:             raise ValueError(
   245:                 f"Weaviate object {oid} missing named vector '{WEAVIATE_NAMED_VECTOR}'. "
   246:                 f"Available keys={sorted(vectors.keys())}"
   247:             )
   248:         token_matrix = np.asarray(vectors[WEAVIATE_NAMED_VECTOR], dtype=np.float32)
   249:         if token_matrix.ndim == 1:
   250:             token_matrix = token_matrix.reshape(1, -1)
   251:         if token_matrix.ndim != 2 or token_matrix.shape[0] <= 0:
   252:             raise ValueError(f"Weaviate vector for {oid} is empty or malformed: shape={token_matrix.shape}")
   253:         if token_matrix.shape[1] != 128:
   254:             raise ValueError(
   255:                 f"Weaviate vector for {oid} must be 128-d, got shape={token_matrix.shape}."
   256:             )
   257:         out[oid] = token_matrix
   258: 
   259:     missing = [oid for oid in ids if oid not in out]
   260:     if missing:
   261:         raise RuntimeError(f"Failed to fetch vectors for object ids: {missing}")
   262:     return out
   263: 
   264: 
   265: def _rows_to_hits(items: list[dict]) -> list[ClaimHit]:
   266:     hits: list[ClaimHit] = []
   267:     for it in items:
   268:         addl = it.get("_additional") or {}
   269:         hits.append(
   270:             ClaimHit(
   271:                 uuid=addl.get("id", ""),
   272:                 distance=addl.get("distance"),
   273:                 claim_id=it.get("claim_id", ""),
   274:                 doc_id=it.get("doc_id", ""),
   275:             )
   276:         )
   277:     return hits
   278: 
   279: 
   280: def _claim_key(hit: ClaimHit) -> str:
   281:     return str(hit.claim_id or hit.uuid or hit.doc_id or "")
   282:

   386: 
   387: def _maxsim_score(query_tokens: np.ndarray, doc_tokens: np.ndarray) -> float:
   388:     if doc_tokens.size == 0 or query_tokens.size == 0:
   389:         return float("-inf")
   390:     qt = query_tokens.astype(np.float32, copy=False)
   391:     dt = doc_tokens.astype(np.float32, copy=False)
   392:     sims = qt @ dt.T
   393:     return float(np.max(sims, axis=1).sum())
   394: 
   395: 
   396: def _fmt_score(value: float | None) -> str:
   397:     return "" if value is None else f"{value:.6f}"
   398: 
   399: 
   400: def rerank_with_lmdb(
   401:     hits: list[ClaimHit],
   402:     query: str,
   403:     shard: str,
   404:     rerank_k: int = 100,
   405:     query_tokens: np.ndarray | None = None,
   406: ) -> list[ClaimHit]:
   407:     shard = assert_128_variant(shard, context="rerank_with_lmdb")
   408:     q_tokens = query_tokens if query_tokens is not None else _embed_query_tokens(query, shard)
   409:     top = hits[: int(rerank_k)]
   410:     rescored: list[ClaimHit] = []
   411:     for h in top:
   412:         lmdb_path = resolve_lmdb_path(shard, doc_id=h.doc_id or None)
   413:         doc_tokens = load_colbert_from_lmdb(lmdb_path, h.claim_id)
   414:         if doc_tokens is None:
   415:             raise RuntimeError(f"Missing LMDB vectors for claim_id={h.claim_id} at {lmdb_path}")
   416:         if np.asarray(doc_tokens).ndim != 2 or np.asarray(doc_tokens).shape[1] != 128:
   417:             raise ValueError(
   418:                 f"LMDB vectors for claim_id={h.claim_id} must be [T,128], got {np.asarray(doc_tokens).shape}"
   419:             )
   420:         h.score = _maxsim_score(q_tokens, doc_tokens)
   421:         rescored.append(h)
   422:     rescored.sort(key=lambda x: (x.score if x.score is not None else float("-inf")), reverse=True)
   423:     return rescored + hits[int(rerank_k) :]
   424:

   453: def rerank_hits(

   455:     query: str,

   456:     shard: str,
   457:     rerank_k: int = 100,
   458:     *,
   459:     rerank_source: str = DEFAULT_RERANK_SOURCE,
   460:     query_tokens: np.ndarray | None = None,
   461: ) -> list[ClaimHit]:
   462:     source = _normalize_rerank_source(rerank_source)
   463:     if source == "lmdb":
   464:         return rerank_with_lmdb(hits, query, shard=shard, rerank_k=rerank_k, query_tokens=query_tokens)
   465:     return rerank_with_weaviate(hits, query, shard=shard, rerank_k=rerank_k, query_tokens=query_tokens)
   466: 
   467: 
   468: def debug_compare_lmdb_vs_weaviate_vectors(
   469:     hits: list[ClaimHit],
   470:     shard: str,
   471:     sample_n: int,
   472:     *,
   473:     max_abs_tol: float = 0.05,
   474: ) -> dict[str, float]:
   475:     shard = assert_128_variant(shard, context="debug_compare_lmdb_vs_weaviate_vectors")
   476:     sample_pool = [h for h in hits if h.uuid and h.claim_id]
   477:     if not sample_pool:
   478:         raise ValueError("No candidates with both uuid and claim_id available for vector comparison.")
   479:     n = min(int(sample_n), len(sample_pool))
   480:     sampled = random.sample(sample_pool, n)
   481:     obj_ids = [h.uuid for h in sampled]
   482:     weaviate_vecs = fetch_colbert_vectors_from_weaviate(obj_ids)
   483:     diffs: list[float] = []
   484:     for h in sampled:
   485:         lmdb_path = resolve_lmdb_path(shard, doc_id=h.doc_id or None)
   486:         lmdb_vec = load_colbert_from_lmdb(lmdb_path, h.claim_id)
   487:         if lmdb_vec is None:
   488:             raise RuntimeError(f"LMDB vector missing for claim_id={h.claim_id}")
   489:         wv_vec = weaviate_vecs.get(h.uuid)
   490:         if wv_vec is None:
   491:             raise RuntimeError(f"Weaviate vector missing for object_id={h.uuid}")
   492:         lmdb_arr = np.asarray(lmdb_vec, dtype=np.float32)
   493:         if lmdb_arr.ndim == 1:
   494:             lmdb_arr = lmdb_arr.reshape(1, -1)
   495:         if lmdb_arr.shape != wv_vec.shape:
   496:             raise ValueError(
   497:                 f"Shape mismatch claim_id={h.claim_id}: lmdb={lmdb_arr.shape} weaviate={wv_vec.shape}"
   498:             )
   499:         max_abs = float(np.max(np.abs(lmdb_arr - wv_vec)))
   500:         diffs.append(max_abs)
   501:     stats = {
   502:         "n": float(n),
   503:         "max_abs_min": float(np.min(diffs)),
   504:         "max_abs_mean": float(np.mean(diffs)),
   505:         "max_abs_p95": float(np.percentile(diffs, 95)),
   506:         "max_abs_max": float(np.max(diffs)),
   507:     }
   508:     print(
   509:         "[debug-compare] n={n} max_abs(min/mean/p95/max)="
   510:         "{minv:.6f}/{meanv:.6f}/{p95:.6f}/{maxv:.6f}".format(
   511:             n=int(stats["n"]),
   512:             minv=stats["max_abs_min"],
   513:             meanv=stats["max_abs_mean"],
   514:             p95=stats["max_abs_p95"],
   515:             maxv=stats["max_abs_max"],
   516:         )
   517:     )
   518:     if stats["max_abs_max"] > float(max_abs_tol):
   519:         raise ValueError(
   520:             f"LMDB vs Weaviate vector mismatch too large: max_abs={stats['max_abs_max']:.6f} > {max_abs_tol}"
   521:         )
   522:     return stats
   523: 
   524: 
   525: def lookup_patent(doc_id: str, limit: int = 200) -> list[ClaimHit]:
   526:     where = (
   527:         "{path:[\"doc_id\"],operator:Equal,valueText:"
   528:         f"\"{doc_id}\"}}"
   529:     )
   530:     gql = (
   531:         "{ Get { Claim("
   532:         f"where:{where}, limit:{int(limit)}"
   533:         ") { claim_id doc_id _additional { id } } } }"
   534:     )
   535:     data = _post_graphql(gql)
   536:     items = data["Get"]["Claim"]
   537:     hits: list[ClaimHit] = []
   538:     for it in items:
   539:         addl = it.get("_additional") or {}
   540:         hits.append(
   541:             ClaimHit(
   542:                 uuid=addl.get("id", ""),

   794:         q = row.get("query", "")
   795:         if not q:
   796:             continue
   797:         print(f"[eval] {i}/{total_q} retrieving + reranking")
   798:         rel = qrels.get(q, set())
   799:         hits = retrieve_claims(
   800:             q,
   801:             limit=limit,
   802:             shard=retrieve_shard,
   803:             retrieval_mode=retrieval_mode,
   804:             hybrid_alpha=hybrid_alpha,
   805:         )
   806:         candidate_ids = [h.claim_id for h in hits]
   807:         candidate_ranks = [idx for idx, cid in enumerate(candidate_ids, start=1) if cid in rel]
   808:         candidate_hit = bool(candidate_ranks)
   809:         q_tokens = _embed_query_tokens(q, rerank_shard)
   810:         claim_id_to_uuid = {h.claim_id: h.uuid for h in hits if h.claim_id and h.uuid}
   811:         weaviate_vec_cache: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
   812:         maxsim_cache: dict[str, float | None] = {}
   813: 
   814:         def _claim_maxsim(claim_id: str) -> float | None:
   815:             if not claim_id:
   816:                 return None
   817:             cached = maxsim_cache.get(claim_id)
   818:             if cached is not None or claim_id in maxsim_cache:
   819:                 return cached
   820:             if rerank_source == "weaviate":
   821:                 obj_id = claim_id_to_uuid.get(claim_id)
   822:                 if not obj_id:
   823:                     maxsim_cache[claim_id] = None
   824:                     return None
   825:                 if obj_id not in weaviate_vec_cache:
   826:                     fetched = fetch_colbert_vectors_from_weaviate([obj_id])
   827:                     weaviate_vec_cache.update(fetched)
   828:                 doc_tokens = weaviate_vec_cache.get(obj_id)
   829:                 if doc_tokens is None:
   830:                     raise RuntimeError(
   831:                         f"Missing Weaviate vectors for object_id={obj_id} claim_id={claim_id}"
   832:                     )
   833:             else:
   834:                 lmdb_path = resolve_lmdb_path(rerank_shard)
   835:                 doc_tokens = load_colbert_from_lmdb(lmdb_path, claim_id)
   836:                 if doc_tokens is None:
   837:                     maxsim_cache[claim_id] = None
   838:                     return None
   839:             arr = np.asarray(doc_tokens)
   840:             if arr.ndim == 1:
   841:                 arr = arr.reshape(1, -1)
   842:             if arr.ndim != 2 or arr.shape[1] != 128:
   843:                 raise ValueError(f"MaxSim vectors must be [T,128], got shape={arr.shape} for claim_id={claim_id}")
   844:             score = _maxsim_score(q_tokens, doc_tokens)
   845:             maxsim_cache[claim_id] = score
   846:             return score
   847: 
   848:         ranked = rerank_hits(
   849:             hits,
   850:             q,
   851:             shard=rerank_shard,
   852:             rerank_k=rerank_k,
   853:             rerank_source=rerank_source,
   854:             query_tokens=q_tokens,
   855:         )
   856:         if debug_compare_vectors and rerank_source == "weaviate":
   857:             debug_compare_lmdb_vs_weaviate_vectors(
   858:                 ranked[: max(1, rerank_k)],
   859:                 rerank_shard,
   860:                 sample_n=debug_compare_n,
   861:                 max_abs_tol=debug_compare_max_abs_tol,
   862:             )
   863:         ranked_ids = [h.claim_id for h in ranked]
   864:         p10 = precision_at_k(ranked_ids, rel, 10)
   865:         r10 = recall_at_k(ranked_ids, rel, 10)
   866:         n10 = ndcg_at_k(ranked_ids, rel, 10)

  1010: def _write_hits_csv(hits: list[ClaimHit], path: Path, k: int = 10):

    58:     parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=50, help="Retrieval candidate limit.")
    59:     args = parser.parse_args()
    60: 
    61:     unique = uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
    62:     claims = _build_smoke_claims(unique)
    63:     query = f"smoke-key-{unique}"
    64: 
    65:     embedded = embed_chunks(claims, tokenizer, model)
    66:     if not embedded:
    67:         raise RuntimeError("Embedding returned no records.")
    68:     token_matrix = np.asarray(embedded[0].get("colbert"))
    69:     if token_matrix.ndim == 1:
    70:         token_matrix = token_matrix.reshape(1, -1)
    71:     if token_matrix.ndim != 2 or token_matrix.shape[1] != 128:
    72:         raise ValueError(f"Expected embedded token vectors [T,128], got {token_matrix.shape}")
    73: 
    74:     store_embeddings(embedded)
    75: 
    76:     hits = retrieve_claims(query, limit=args.limit, shard="128_f16", retrieval_mode="vector")
    77:     expected_claim_ids = {c["claim_id"] for c in claims}
    78:     smoke_hits = [h for h in hits if h.claim_id in expected_claim_ids]
    79:     if not smoke_hits:
    80:         raise RuntimeError("Inserted smoke claims were not retrieved from Weaviate.")
    81: 
    82:     object_ids = [h.uuid for h in smoke_hits]
    83:     if any(not oid for oid in object_ids):
    84:         raise RuntimeError("Smoke retrieval hit missing Weaviate UUID.")
    85:     vectors = fetch_colbert_vectors_from_weaviate(object_ids)
    86:     for oid in object_ids:
    87:         arr = vectors.get(oid)
    88:         if arr is None:
    89:             raise RuntimeError(f"Missing fetched vector for object_id={oid}")
    90:         if arr.ndim != 2 or arr.shape[1] != 128:
    91:             raise ValueError(f"Fetched Weaviate vectors must be [T,128], got {arr.shape}")
    92: 
    93:     reranked = rerank_hits(
    94:         smoke_hits,
    95:         query,
    96:         shard="128_f16",
    97:         rerank_k=len(smoke_hits),
    98:         rerank_source="weaviate",
    99:     )
   100:     if not reranked or reranked[0].score is None:
   101:         raise RuntimeError("Weaviate-based reranking did not produce scores.")
   102: 
   103:     print(
   104:         "[smoke] success: embedded_128=1 inserted=1 fetched_vectors=1 reranked=1 "
   105:         f"top_claim_id={reranked[0].claim_id} top_score={reranked[0].score:.6f}"
   106:     )
   107: 
   108: 
   109: if __name__ == "__main__":
   110:     main()

    21: public static class SleepBlock {
    22:   [DllImport("kernel32.dll")]
    23:   public static extern uint SetThreadExecutionState(uint esFlags);
    24: }
    25: "@ | Out-Null
    26: 
    27:     $ES_CONTINUOUS = [Convert]::ToUInt32("80000000", 16)
    28:     $ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED = [Convert]::ToUInt32("00000001", 16)
    29:     $ES_AWAYMODE_REQUIRED = [Convert]::ToUInt32("00000040", 16)
    30:     $flags = [uint32]($ES_CONTINUOUS -bor $ES_SYSTEM_REQUIRED -bor $ES_AWAYMODE_REQUIRED)
    31:     [SleepBlock]::SetThreadExecutionState($flags) | Out-Null
    32: }
    33: 
    34: function Disable-KeepAwake {
    35:     $ES_CONTINUOUS = [Convert]::ToUInt32("80000000", 16)
    36:     try {
    37:         [SleepBlock]::SetThreadExecutionState($ES_CONTINUOUS) | Out-Null
    38:     }
    39:     catch {
    40:     }
    41: }
    42: 
    43: try {
    44:     if ($CleanOutputs) {
    45:         Remove-Item -Recurse -Force "backend\lmdb\colbert_128_f16.lmdb" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    46:         Remove-Item -Recurse -Force "backend\lmdb\colbert_128_f32.lmdb" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    47:         Remove-Item -Recurse -Force "backend\lmdb\patent_metadata.lmdb" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    48:         Remove-Item -Recurse -Force "backend\lmdb\claim_payloads.lmdb" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    49:         Remove-Item "backend\validation\master_patent_manifest.csv" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    50:         Remove-Item "backend\validation\ingest_completed_dates.txt" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    51:     }
    52: 
    53:     if ($ResetWeaviate) {
    54:         Push-Location "backend\app"
    55:         try {
    56:             docker compose down -v
    57:             docker compose up -d
    58:         }
    59:         finally {
    60:             Pop-Location
    61:         }
    62:     }
    63: 
    64:     if ($KeepAwake) {
    65:         Enable-KeepAwake
    66:     }
    67: 
    68:     $env:INGEST_MODE = "real-store"
    69:     $env:DROP_FIRST = $(if ($DropFirst) { "1" } else { "0" })
    70:     $env:INGEST_DATES = ""
